BAROTRAUMA RESEARCH NOTES — ORIGINAL FORK (
/home/user/Barotrauma)
Working alongside DNSPY dump (/home/user/BarotraumaMod).
Repo facts:
  - This checkout is the actual Barotrauma source (fork of original game), branch arena/01a03d84-barotrauma.
  - Game source lives under:
      Barotrauma/BarotraumaShared/SharedSource      (shared logic, net structs, afflictions, items, etc.)
      Barotrauma/BarotraumaClient/ClientSource      (client UI/Camera/rendering)
      Barotrauma/BarotraumaServer/ServerSource      (dedicated server authority)
    + Barotrauma/BarotraumaTest/ClientServer
  - BarotraumaMod (separate checkout) is the same game decompiled via dnSpy; useful as cross-reference and
    for exact line numbers / field metadata ([NetworkSerialize] etc.).
  - Research tracker here: research/paths.csv, research/ALL_CS_PATHS.txt, research/RESEARCH_LOG.txt.
[WAVE 2 — ORIGINAL SOURCE CONFIRMATION + NEW BUG HUNT]

    - NetworkHeader enum lines ~21-30
    - struct NetAffliction { Identifier; ushort Strength; int VitalityDecrease; ushort Price; } lines 57-78
    - SetAffliction: Price = (ushort)(BaseHealCost + Strength*HealCostMultiplier)  // REAL price only when server builds the list
    - HealAllPending lines 193-216:
        int totalCost = GetTotalCost();
        if (!(campaign?.TryPurchase(client, totalCost) ?? false)) return InsufficientFunds;
        ... ReduceAfflictionOnAllLimbs(affliction.Identifier, affliction.Prefab?.MaxStrength ?? affliction.Strength)
    - GetTotalCost line 320 = sum of client-supplied affliction.Price
  Server: Barotrauma/BarotraumaServer/ServerSource/GameSession/MedicalClinic.cs
    - ServerRead switch (ADD_PENDING=4, HEAL_PENDING=7, etc.)

    - CampaignMode.cs line 276: return price == 0 || GetWallet(client).TryDeduct(price);
    - MultiPlayerCampaign.cs line 1507 override; price==0 -> true; otherwise wallet.TryDeduct (or bank if manager).
NEW BUG #1 — ARBITRARY MONEY TRANSFER VOTE (bypass MaximumMoneyTransferRequest / wallet permissions)
File: Barotrauma/BarotraumaServer/ServerSource/Networking/Voting.cs
Lines 318-340, case VoteType.TransferMoney:
    bool startVote = inc.ReadBoolean();
    if (startVote)
    {
        int amount = inc.ReadInt32();
        int fromClientId = inc.ReadByte();
        int toClientId = inc.ReadByte();
        if (!ShouldRejectVote(sender, voteType))
        {
            pendingVotes.Enqueue(new TransferVote(sender,
                ConnectedClients.Find(c => c.SessionId == fromClientId),
                amount,
                ConnectedClients.Find(c => c.SessionId == toClientId)));
        }
    }
WHAT IS MISSING HERE:
  - No check amount <= ServerSettings.MaximumMoneyTransferRequest
  - No check that from = bank only when sender can manage wallets; from can be ANY player wallet
  - No check that to is sender (a normal player can set to anyone)
  - Non-manager normal player normally reaches money vote via MultiPlayerCampaign.ServerReadMoney
    (lines 1295+), which enforces the above. This Voting.ServerRead path is a second, weaker entry and
    is reachable via ClientNetSegment.Vote (GameServer.cs lines 1311/1486).
NORMAL CLIENT NEVER SENDS startVote=true for TransferMoney:
  - Barotrauma/BarotraumaClient/ClientSource/Networking/Voting.cs line ~299:
      case VoteType.TransferMoney:
          if (data is not int money) return false;
          msg.WriteBoolean(false); // not initiating a vote
          msg.WriteInt32(money);
  => a crafted client packet is the only way to execute this branch.
IMPACT:
  - Start a vote to transfer ANY amount (up to wallet balance) from Bank or another player to yourself,
    bypassing MaximumMoneyTransferRequest and the "receiver must be self" rule.
  - TransferVote.Finish (lines 51-71): fromWallet.TryDeduct(amount); toWallet?.Give(amount).
  - Can also enqueue unlimited pending TransferVotes with only ShouldRejectVote cooldown (minor queue spam).
NEW BUG #2 (minor / server robustness) — request malformed values cause index errors:
  - EventManager.ServerRead: selectedOption is inc.ReadByte(), then convAction.Options[selectedOption]
    (EventManager.cs line ~ 99). Out-of-range option (not 255) -> IndexOutOfRange.
  - Inventory.SharedRead (SharedSource/Items/Inventory.cs line ~1108): start/end are bytes with no
    clamp against capacity; receivedItemIds[i] can be OOB -> IndexOutOfRange.
  Not high value, but worth knowing for server-crash / desync fuzzing.
NEW BUG #3 (interesting, low impact): client can ChangeProperty on items they can access.
  - Item.ServerEventRead -> EventType.ChangeProperty -> ReadPropertyChange.
  - ReadPropertyChange (SharedSource/Items/Item.cs line ~3855) with inGameEditableOnly=true.
  - GetInGameEditableProperties(ignoreConditions:true) returns all ConditionallyEditable+InGameEditable.
  - Server then re-checks ConditionallyEditable.IsEditable(this).
  - ConditionType.OnlyByStatusEffectsAndNetwork returns GameMain.NetworkMember is {IsServer:true}
    (ConditionallyEditable.cs line ~65). So on a dedicated server these are editable:
        Item.DescriptionTag, ItemComponent.LockGuiFramePosition, ItemComponent.GuiFrameOffset.
  - CanClientAccess(sender) required. Impact is cosmetic-ish, but demonstrates the server trusts
    network property changes granted by an attribute intended for StatusEffects.
REJECTED candidates this wave (verified no bypass):
  - Fabricator: client sends recipeHash/amount, but CanBeFabricated checks CanAfford for RequiredMoney
    before Fabricate(); TryPurchase return value is ignored only AFTER CanAfford passed. No free craft.
  - Store: ReadPurchasedItems quantity clamped 0..100, DeliverImmediately gated, prices server-side.
  - Upgrades/item swaps: TryPurchaseUpgrade/PurchaseItemSwap called with force=false, price server-side.
  - ServerSettings.ServerRead: needs ManageSettings.
  - Event responses: TargetClients must contain sender.
  - Hull fire/water edit: needs ConsoleCommands + fire/editfire permission.
  - Inventory: accessibility check via IsInventoryAccessible + item.CanClientAccess.
  - TakeOverBot/hiring: hire charges through TryHireCharacter.
  - CircuitBox: CanAccessAndUnlocked + required items.
  - Wallet direct transfer (ServerReadMoney): amount<=0 return, from/from, manager checks + max amount.
[NEXT TARGETS]
  - Read EventManager option bounds patch / crash vector.
  - Check ServerEntityEventManager + Hull for water/fire DoS.
  - Look at Skill/stat updates over network (maybe client can send stats).
  - Examine ability/status effects triggered by items components (Terminal, CustomInterface) for
    ability injection.
[WAVE 3 — ABILITY FLAGS verdict (+ rejection note)]
Files read: SharedSource/Enums.cs (653-725), Character.cs (6075-6120),
CharacterHealth.cs (130-155,650-690), CharacterAbilityGiveFlag.cs, CharacterAbilityModifyFlag.cs.
VERDICT: AbilityFlags are NOT directly network-writable from a client.
- enum AbilityFlags: MustWalk=0x1, ImmuneToPressure=0x2, IgnoredByEnemyAI=0x4,
  MoveNormallyWhileDragging=0x8, CanTinker=0x10, CanTinkerFabricatorsAndDeconstructors=0x20,
  TinkeringPowersDevices=0x40, GainSkillPastMaximum=0x80, RetainExperienceForNewCharacter=0x100,
  AllowSecondOrderedTarget=0x200, AlwaysStayConscious=0x400, CanNotDieToAfflictions=0x800.
- Character.AddAbilityFlag/RemoveAbilityFlag/HasAbilityFlag are plain private-field methods, no
  network serialization. Ability flags are granted by CharacterAbilityGiveFlag /
  CharacterAbilityModifyFlag (talents/abilities) and by missions/status effects.
- Server uses them in authoritative code:
  - CharacterHealth.IsUnconscious uses !HasAbilityFlag(AlwaysStayConscious)
  - CharacterHealth.KillIfOutOfVitality uses !HasAbilityFlag(CanNotDieToAfflictions)
  - Character.IsImmuneToPressure / Movement (MustWalk/MoveNormallyWhileDragging), AI targeting
    (IgnoredByEnemyAI), Repairable tinker gates, CharacterInfo skill gain.
- Since game runs these checks server-side and flags are not read from client packets, a normal
  client cannot "give itself" AlwaysStayConscious / CanNotDieToAfflictions over the wire.
- Potential avenue (not a bug): if a client can somehow grant a talent through UpdateTalents
  (requires CanManageTalents) whose ability is CharacterAbilityGiveFlag, the server itself adds it.
  That gates via CanManageTalents + viability checks, so not directly exploitable.
So AbilityFlags is a good "what actually is client-side" note, but it is NOT a free-heal-class bug.
[WAVE 19 — CROSS-VERIFY USER's "анализ 1/2" HITs AGAINST ORIGINAL SOURCE]
Context: user's Pastebin reports (NTQwFeTe / PiQQtBVP) were written against the DNSPY
dump /home/user/BarotraumaMod. Re-checked every claim in the original fork
/home/user/Barotrauma (authoritative). Lines below are original paths.
VERIFIED TRUE in original:
- HIT #1 Free MedicalClinic (unchanged).
- HIT #2 SERVER_COMMAND outer permission skip:
  GameServer.cs:1739-1753. if (command==ManageRound && mpCampaign!=null) {} else if
  (command==ManageCampaign && mpCampaign!=null) {} else if (!HasPermission) return.
  So in a MultiPlayerCampaign, ManageRound/ManageCampaign reach the switch WITHOUT
  the outer HasPermission check.
  - ManageRound, end=false, continueCampaign=false, !GameStarted, !initiatedStartGame
    -> TryStartGame() with NO inner permission check (lines 1884-1893). ForceStartRound.
  - ManageRound, end=true: inner `mpCampaign == null || AllowedToManageCampaign(...ManageRound)`
    (lines 1815-1816) -> non-admin cannot end a running round, cannot wipe the session
    (mpCampaign==null path is also NOT reachable without ManageRound because the outer
    check applies then). So the "lobby campaign-wipe" claim does NOT hold for a
    non-permission client.
  - ManageRound map LoadNewLevel branch requires ManageCampaign||ManageMap
    (lines 1882/1862) -> NOT a non-perm bypass. The user's "map jump no perms" claim
    is WRONG.
  - ManageCampaign -> mpCampaign.ServerRead (line 1954). Inside ServerRead
    (MultiPlayerCampaign.cs:835+): hull/item repairs/lost shuttle have NO
    HasCampaignInteractionAvailable check (only GetBalance>=cost / TryPurchase).
    -> unprivileged client can remotely trigger these from anywhere, paying from
    their own balance (or bank if they have ManageWallets). Not free, but it is a
    "remote campaign purchase without NPC" weakness.
  - SELECTMODE/SELECTSUB cases also have no permission check in the switch
    (lines 1920-1952) -> lobby tamper, non-critical.
- HIT #3 TAKEOVERBOT:
  GameServer.cs:1583-1592. if (ServerSettings.ReplaceCostPercentage <= 0 ||
  AllowedToManageCampaign(ManageMoney) || AllowedToManageCampaign(ManageHires)).
  Original DEFAULT ReplaceCostPercentage = 100f (ServerSettings.cs:504), NOT 0.
  -> Free hire only when a server sets ReplaceCostPercentage<=0. On default servers the
  permission branch applies (cost = salary*100%, TryHireCharacter checks TryPurchase).
  Also reserve-bench takeover path (line 1611+) only checks AllowBotTakeoverOnPermadeath.
- HIT #4 ReadReadyToSpawn self-set SpectateOnly:
  GameServer.cs:1559-1564. sender.SpectateOnly = bool && (AllowSpectating||Owner);
  sender.WaitForNextRoundRespawn = bool. By design the client controls its own spectator
  flag; RespawnManager.GetClientsToRespawn skips SpectateOnly clients (RespawnManager.cs:22)
  and WaitForNextRoundRespawn can delay respawn (line 62). It is a self-service toggle,
  NOT a privilege escalation. Keep as low-value / design note.
- HIT #7 Force ReadyCheck:
  ReadyCheck.ServerRead (ServerSource/GameSession/ReadyCheck.cs:89-99) accepts
  state=Start when no active ready check -> StartReadyCheck(client.Name, client).
  No server permission check and no server cooldown. Client cooldown
  (ClientSource ReadyCheck.cs:288-297) is bypassable by a crafted packet.
  VERIFIED. Impact: spam 30s ready-check popup to all clients.
- HIT #8 SpectatingPos info leak:
  GameServer.cs:1489-1490 sets c.SpectatePos = vector with no auth. Used in
  ClientWriteIngame (2085-2110): if c.SpectatePos set, any sentient/character whose
  distance to that point is >= DisableDistance is still skipped (culling based on
  spectate point). A live client can send SpectatePos near an arbitrary area to
  receive entity/character state for it -> wallhack-ish info leak. VERIFIED.
- HIT #9 fake-item sell money dup:
  CargoManager.SellItems (ServerSource/GameSession/CargoManager.cs:28-64).
  For Origin=Character the server does NOT require the entity to exist or belong to
  the seller; if FindEntityByID(item.ID) returns null the item is still added and
  store.Balance -= / GetWallet(client).Give(itemValue) runs (lines 55-62). Gated in
  MultiPlayerCampaign.ServerRead by allowedToSellInventoryItems (line 1092/1100) plus
  store.Balance >= value -> effectively admin/self-host or AnyOneAllowedToManageCampaign.
  VERIFIED (privileged-only).
- HIT #10 join without required mods:
  ServerPeer.ReadConnectionInitializationStep, case ContentPackageOrder
  (ServerSource/Networking/Primitives/Peers/Server/ServerPeer.cs:207-208) just sets
  InitializationStep=Success. No client mod list is read or compared to
  contentPackages. VERIFIED.
- HIT #11 File exfiltration:
  FileSender.ReadFileRequest (ServerSource/Networking/FileTransfer/FileSender.cs:336).
  Submarine (382-397): name+hash lookup in SavedSubmarines, no permission check.
  CampaignSave (399-407): no permission check, StartTransfer(GameSession.DataPath).
  Mod (410-430) requires AllowModDownloads + AllPackages. VERIFIED. Default
  AllowFileTransfers=true makes CampaignSave/Submarine downloadable by any client.
REJECTED / CORRECTED in original:
- HIT #6 Fabricator free RequiredMoney: REJECTED. Shared Fabricator.cs:
  Fabricate() (367-372) re-checks CanBeFabricated(); CanBeFabricated (750-777) calls
  mpCampaign.CanAfford(RequiredMoney, GetUsingClient()) and returns false if the client
  cannot afford it. TryPurchase's return value is ignored, but only after CanAfford
  already passed, so the funds ARE deducted (MultiPlayerCampaign.TryPurchase
  lines 1507-1529 TryDeduct/withdraw). No free craft.
  (User's report likely reflects the DNSPY dump's older flow.)
- HIT #2 sub-claim "map jump / session wipe without perms": WRONG. The inner campaign
  checks still require ManageCampaign||ManageMap for LoadNewLevel and
  AllowedToManageCampaign(ManageRound) for end=true.
- HIT #3 sub-claim "default ReplaceCostPercentage==0": WRONG. Original default is 100f.
  Free-hire is server-config dependent (and combined with HIT #2's ManageMoney spoof
  only if the user happens to have ManageMoney via another bug, which is NOT the case).
VOTING.TransferMoney — INDEPENDENT ORIGINAL FINDING (verified this wave again):
- Voting.ServerRead (ServerSource/Networking/Voting.cs:318-340). If startVote=true:
  reads amount (int), fromClientId (byte), toClientId (byte); only gate
  ShouldRejectVote (1-min cooldown). No check against MaximumMoneyTransferRequest,
  no requirement from==Bank when sender lacks ManageWallets, no requirement to==sender.
  TransferVote.Finish (lines 85-97): fromWallet.TryDeduct(amount) -> toWallet?.Give(amount)
  (from/to null mean Bank).
- Normal client never sends startVote=true for TransferMoney
  (ClientSource/Networking/Voting.cs:299-301 writes false).
  => crafted packet can start an arbitrary bank->self / other->self transfer vote,
  bypassing the cap. Needs the vote to pass, so not a "solo instant" exploit; still
  the same class of server-trusts-client bug as MedicalClinic. High priority to
  mention (and fix on a modded server) even though a majority vote is still required.
[WAVE 20 — CROSS-VERIFY USER's "анализ 3" AGAINST ORIGINAL SOURCE]
Context: user's analysis 3 (attached/paste-in) mostly re-derives DNSPY findings and adds
new exploit surfaces. Verified the highest-value original-source claims below.
VERIFIED TRUE (original source /home/user/Barotrauma):
- Item.ReadPropertyChange -> ImpactDamage / ImpactDamageProbability unclamped float:
  Shared/Items/Item.cs:3855-4011. inGameEditableOnly=true on server;
  GetInGameEditableProperties(ignoreConditions:true) includes ConditionallyEditable.
  Server re-checks condition.IsEditable (3859-3877) and CanClientAccess / circuitbox.
  ImpactDamage at Item.cs:472-478 has ConditionallyEditable(ReceivesSubmarineImpacts,
  MinValueFloat,MаxValueFloat) but ReadPropertyChange does NOT apply Min/MaxValueFloat;
  SerializableProperty.TrySetValue (Serialization/SerializableProperty.cs:353+ / 778+)
  just reflects the raw float. ReceiveImpact (Item.cs:2757-2762):
    Rand.Range(0,1) < ImpactDamageProbability; Condition -= impactStrength*ImpactDamage.
  => craftable: ImpactDamage<0 repairs item on impact, Probability>1 always triggers,
  huge value = super fragile. Real trust bug (no wallet/permission, only item access).
- CustomInterface is a separate property-write path (Shared/Items/Components/CustomInterface.cs):
  after CanClientAccess/owner-if-held it calls SetPropertyValueToSignal -> TrySetValue on
  item.AllPropertyObjects, independent of ItemComponent.AllowInGameEditing. Content-dependent
  risk: Powered.PowerConsumption (no clamp) / Powered.MinVoltage (no clamp) via op_clownjukebox
  example. Recreate with a modded/host item packing such a CustomInterface.
- Store reputation/faction bypass:
  CargoManager.PurchaseItems (Shared/GameSession/CargoManager.cs:299-375) checks only
  quantity>0, buyValues[prefab], TryPurchase(client,itemValue). It does NOT call
  ItemPrefab.CanBeBoughtFrom (ItemPrefab.cs:1437+ re-checks MinReputation/RequiredFaction
  /MinLevelDifficulty). Stock quantity in ServerRead is clamped to available stock, but
  faction/reputation gate is not re-enforced. => a low-rep client can buy a
  faction/reputation-gated item if it is physically in stock and they can afford it.
  (UI normally hides these via CanBeBoughtFrom.)
- Steering autopilot maintain-pos unclamped:
  ServerSource/Items/Components/Machines/Steering.cs:46-56 reads two raw floats into
  posToMaintain, no MathUtils.IsValid/clamp; only item.CanClientAccess(c) after.
  Client UI limits maintain position to sonar/range; server does not.
- OrderManualPriority no clamp:
  Shared/Networking/OrderChatMessage.cs:154-225 reads byte 0..255; ChatMessage.ServerRead
  (ServerSources/Networking/ChatMessage.cs:60-71) calls .WithManualPriority(priority);
  Order.WithManualPriority (Shared/Characters/AI/Order.cs:725+) just passes int.
  HighestManualOrderPriority == 3 (CharacterInfo.cs:590). => craft orders with priority up
  to 255 to preempt normal bot orders.
- OrderChatMessage missing ContainsKey (malformed):
  Shared/Networking/OrderChatMessage.cs:81 and :183 do OrderPrefab.Prefabs[id]
  without TryGetValue. Bad id => KeyNotFoundException (chat path).
- Order wallSectionIndex OOB:
  Shared/Characters/AI/Order.cs:532 Structure.Sections[WallSectionIndex.Value] with
  no bounds check after ReadOrder byte (OrderChatMessage.cs:220). => log-spam exception.
- TriggerComponent ApplyForce bug:
  Shared/Items/Components/TriggerComponent.cs:488-489:
    if (!HorizontalForce) { force.Y = 0f; }
    if (!VerticalForce)   { force.Y = 0f; }
  Second line should be force.X. So HorizontalForce=false incorrectly zeroes Y (twice),
  leaving X force active. Also set_force (522-527) accepts any float via FloatTryParse
  (no clamp / IsValid) and Width/Height/Radius/BodyOffset rebuild the trigger runtime.
- ServerPeer max-players / pending race:
  ServerSource/.../ServerPeer.cs:266-277: when connectedClients>=MaxPlayers it calls
  RemovePendingClient(ServerFull) but does NOT return; if the pending client already has
  InitializationStep==Success it is still promoted + OnInitializationComplete. Combined
  with LidgrenServerPeer.HandleConnection (190-260) checking only connectedClients.Count
  (not pending), this is a race for extra/beyond max joins (not a solo guaranteed exploit).
- Name collision only checks ConnectedClients:
  ServerPeer.cs:159-166 name taken search ignores other pendingClients => duplicate/homoglyph
  join race in lobby.
- GameServer SelectMode raw modeIndex OOB:
  GameServer.cs:1941-1949 reads ushort, sets SelectedModeIndex (clamped) then directly
  GameModes[modeIndex] => ArgumentOutOfRange/log-spam lobby surface.
- P2POwnerDoSProtection wrong per-endpoint count:
  ClientSource/Utils/P2POwnerDoSProtection.cs:71-74: in the time-window loop it calls
  CheckForExcessivePackets(e, count) passing the CURRENT endpoint's count for EVERY e.
  => one noisy P2P peer can falsely kick/ban unrelated endpoints.
- P2POwnerPeer unauthenticated queue unbounded:
  ClientSource/.../P2POwnerPeer.cs:172-174 copies every packet into UnauthedMessages while
  AuthenticationPending with no count/size limit (auth-stage memory DoS).
- AccountInfo asymmetric Equals:
  Shared/Networking/Primitives/AccountInfo.cs:36-40 tests
  this.OtherMatchingIds ⊆ other.OtherMatchingIds only, not the reverse; GetHashCode uses
  only AccountId => asymmetric equality for mixed alias sets.
- FileSender CampaignSave/Submarine exfil (already Wave 19, re-confirmed here).
NOT independently exploitable / design notes:
- Sonar UseMineralScanner is accepted on server but unlock requires
  HasMineralScanner client-side; not a server trust bug by itself.
- Reactor/PowerContainer/PowerDistributor/Pump/Engine/CircuitBox/Character+TalentTree
  were re-confirmed tightly gated; no free money/power/talent path.
- Repairable/Deconstructor/ItemContainer/Connection/Pickable/etc. give useful modding
  primitives, not client-trust holes.

9. Malformed/log-spam vectors: Inventory.SharedRead start>end; Fabricator unknown

   wallSectionIndex; GameServer SelectMode modeIndex; Item component unchecked cast.

[WAVE 21 — HUNT #1: UNEXPLORED SERVER ITEM COMPONENTS + ENTITY EVENT BUFFER]
NEW STRONG FINDING — ServerEntityEventManager.Read unbounded msgLength (crash/DoS):
File: Barotrauma/BarotraumaServer/ServerSource/Networking/NetEntityEvent/ServerEntityEventManager.cs,
Read() lines 475-520. For each entity event the server reads:
    int msgLength = (int)msg.ReadVariableUInt32();
    ...
    byte[] temp = msg.ReadBytes(msgLength - 2);   // client-controlled size
There is NO validation that msgLength >= 2 or <= remaining packet bytes.
- If msgLength < 2 -> msgLength-2 is negative -> new byte[negative] (Overflow/ArgumentOutOfRange).
- If msgLength is large (near int.MaxValue) -> huge allocation attempt (OOM-ish).
- If entity == null, it does msg.BitPosition += msgLength * 8 (untrusted huge shift).
GameServer.ReadDataMessage wraps only dosProtection.Start(connectedClient); there is no
try/catch around ClientReadIngame -> entityEventManager.Read(inc, c) (GameServer.cs:1484).
=> Crafted EntityState packet can turn valid entityID + bogus msgLength into
exception / large allocation on the server. This is a NEW malformed/DoS vector not in
the user's three analyses. Severity: high for a malicious packet; server crash depends on
how the top-level Lidgren handler treats exceptions.
RE-READ item components in original (all small, all now logged full):
- DockingPort.ServerEventRead: only outpost auto-docking; requires AllowedToManageCampaign
  (ManageMap). Safe.
- Deconstructor.ServerEventRead: bool active, gated item.CanClientAccess(c), then SetActive.
  Safe (amounts/recipe server-side).
- Engine.ServerEventRead: ReadRangedInteger(-10,10)*10 -> -100..100; CanClientAccess. Safe.
- Pump.ServerEventRead: ReadRangedInteger(-10,10)*10, bool active; CanClientAccess. Safe.
- Reactor.ServerEventRead: autoTemp/powerOn/fission/turbine/temperatureBoost all ranged
  (RangedSingle(0,100,8) and (-Boost,+Boost,8)); CanClientAccess; blameOnBroken set for
  dangerous manual overrides (karma attribution). No money/fuel exploit.
- PowerContainer.ServerEventRead: ReadRangedInteger(0,10)/10*maxRechargeSpeed; CanClientAccess.
  Client cannot set charge/capacity/output. Safe.
- PowerDistributor.ServerEventRead: SharedEventRead; only NameChange/RatioChange;
  CanClientAccess. Safe.
- ButtonTerminal.ServerEventRead: ReadRangedInteger(0, Signals.Length-1), CanClientAccess,
  SendSignal. Safe.
- WifiComponent.ServerEventRead: Channel + 10 channelMemory all ReadRangedInteger(Min,Max);
  CanClientAccess. Safe; useful 10x synced int slots for mods.
- Wire.ServerEventRead: nodeCount = ReadByte() (no bound), lastNodePos = 2 raw floats
  (no MathUtils.IsValid/clamp); CanClientAccess. Weak point: arbitrary wire node coords /
  node truncation; wire nodes are used for rendering/link geometry, not a physical exploit
  on its own. Logged as minor.
- ConnectionPanel.ServerEventRead: CONFIRMED malformed vector. wires[] is a
  List<Wire>[Connections.Count]; it fills only i < min(Connections.Count, connectionCount).
  Later loops every i in 0..Connections.Count and does `wires[i].Contains(...)`/foreach
  -> if connectionCount < Connections.Count, wires[i] is null -> NullReferenceException.
  Gated by !Locked && !TemporarilyLocked && AllowRewiring && item.CanClientAccess(c).
  => crafted small connectionCount on an accessible unlocked panel = exception/log-spam.
- Terminal.ServerEventRead: reads string, CanClientAccess + !Readonly, truncates to
  MaxMessageLength, writes to display/signal_out. Safe (length capped).
CUSTOM INTERFACE -> POWERED (originally from analysis 3, now re-verified in original):
- Server CustomInterface.ServerEventRead reads raw strings/bools per element; no
  min/max/step enforcement; ValueChanged/TextChanged call
  SetPropertyValueToSignal -> (Shared CustomInterface.cs:296-315) item.AllPropertyObjects
  SerializableProperties[PropertyName].TrySetValue(po, element.Signal). This bypasses
  ItemComponent.AllowInGameEditing.
- Powered.PowerConsumption setter (Powered.cs:132-133) and MinVoltage (125-127) have no
  clamp; UpdateGrids (500-570): if GetCurrentPowerConsumption()<0 the connection is added
  as a power source (AddSrc). So a content item with
  CustomInterface propertyname="PowerConsumption" targetitemcomponent="Powered" can be
  driven negative by any nearby client -> that device becomes a free power source.
  Also MinVoltage negative -> HasPower true at any Voltage. Content-dependent but real.
CHARACTER SERVER EVENT READ (now logged 189-349):
- ServerReadInput: inputCount byte read but only i<60 processed; networkUpdateID monotonic;
  newAim/newInteract read only for relevant flags. No obvious exploit; large inputCount
  just consumes bytes (buffer bounds not checked per iteration — minor malformed surface).
- ServerEventRead: EventType.Status allows client controlling an incapacitated character to
  Kill (legit give-up/kill-self), Treatment toggles CPR, UpdateTalents/ConfirmTalentRefund
  require CanManageTalents (only self or bot with ManageBotTalents) + IsViableTalentForCharacter.
  No free talent/skill/money path.
Message.cs MsgReader.ReadBytes (427-431): `new byte[numberOfBytes]` with number from packet
before any length validation -> this is what makes the ServerEntityEventManager msgLength
vector real.
[WAVE 22 — Item/Inventory/Hull server event surfaces (original, full read)]
Item.ServerEventRead (BarotraumaServer/ServerSource/Items/Item.cs:185-233):
- EventType.ComponentState: (components[componentIndex] as IClientSerializable).ServerEventRead
  with NO null check -> if the targeted component is not IClientSerializable, NRE. The index is
  ranged, but the cast is not validated -> generic malformed vector (log-spam).

  Combine(combineTarget, c.Character). Quantity/result are server-side. No obvious dupe;
  normal in-game combine.
- EventType.ChangeProperty: the ImpactDamage/Rotation/CustomInterface-surface already found.
Inventory.ServerEventRead + SharedRead (Server Inventory.cs, Shared Inventory.cs):
- SharedRead: byte start/end are used directly to index receivedItemIds[i] with NO
  `end <= capacity` / `i < length` check (Shared Inventory.cs:1108-1140). start=200,end=210
  -> IndexOutOfRangeException. Confirmed malformed vector (log-spam).

  GetItemAccessibility(), Pickable check for added items, handcuff special-case, karma for
  taking from other player inventory. No remote item-steal/dupe found.
Hull.ServerEventRead (Server Hull.cs:127-268, Shared 868-880):
- Status (water/fire): gated by ConsoleCommands + permitted "fire"/"editfire" commands
  (permission only). Confirmed moderator/admin power, not a normal-player exploit.
- BackgroundSections: sectionToUpdate ranged ReadRangedInteger(0,Count-1); sprayer + liquid
  color + distance ~ Range*1.1 + color-towards-paint sanity checks. No OOB.
- Decal: decalIndex byte, checked < decals.Count; requires character.AllowInput + a held
  Sprayer. Safe.

- Holdable.ServerEventRead: CanClientAccess + Attachable + !attached + MathUtils.IsValid +

- Fabricator.ServerEventRead: StartFabricating(fabricationRecipes[recipeHash]) with no

- Repairable.ServerEventRead -> RepairBoost(QTESuccess):
  Server READ (Repairable server) may call RepairBoost from "None" action; RepairBoost
  (Shared Repairable.cs:313-327) only checks CurrentFixer != null. It does NOT verify
  CurrentFixer == sender. Combined with no server-side cooldown on RepairBoost, a client who
  is NOT the current fixer but has access + required items can repeatedly send
  {action=None, QTESuccess=true} and apply QTE-style condition increase (repair) or

  perks if NO OTHER connected client has ManageSettings (designed "no admin online"

[WAVE 25 — ChatMessage rest, BanList, Voip]
- ChatMessage.ServerRead (rest, 140-262): normal chat uses SanitizeText + MaxLength +
  Levenshtein spam filter + ChatSpamSpeed + kick after >3 spam. Sender name comes from c.Name,
  not the packet. Order path was covered in Wave 20. No injection/trust hole (order
  identifier/wallSectionIndex OOB remain the only crash vectors there).
- BanList.ServerAdminRead (341-370): if no Ban permission it skips removeCount*4 bytes and
  returns false; if Ban permission, unban only with Unban permission. removeCount is UInt32;
  the no-permission skip does `BitPosition += (int)removeCount*4*8` without checking the
  remaining bytes -> huge removeCount can push seekPos out of range (minor malformed vector,
  only affects clients without Ban perm). Not a privilege bypass.
- VoipQueue.Read (136-175): BUFFER_COUNT(B) lengths are bytes 0..255 -> per packet the
  server can be told to ReadBytes(total up to 8*255=2040 bytes) while a small packet is
  sent. No bounds check against remaining message length; a crafted voice packet can cause
  ReadBytes to read/allocate beyond the message (exception/log-spam) and it is the classic
  small-amplification case (max ~2KB per packet). Low severity but worth noting; Voice
  queue state can also be forced by sending a newer LatestBufferID (relatively cheap to
  invalidate the active voice windows). Marked.
[WAVE 26 — ReadDataMessage dispatcher sweep + new crash vectors]
ReadDataMessage (GameServer.cs:824-1105) reviewed all 27 ClientPacketHeader cases.
New CRASH/DoS vector #2 — CIRCUITBOX direct packet (packet 21):
- ReadCircuitBoxMessage (GameServer.cs ~1530-1543):
    var header = INetSerializableStruct.Read<NetCircuitBoxHeader>(inc);
    INetSerializableStruct data = header.Opcode switch
    {
        CircuitBoxOpcode.Cursor => ...,
        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(...)
    };
- NetCircuitBoxHeader.Opcode is [NetworkSerialize] enum (CircuitBoxNetStructs.cs:45);
  INetSerializableStruct.ReadEnum (INetSerializableStruct.cs:328-345) uses
  GetEnumRange + bitField.ReadInteger(range) which CLAMPS to a valid enum value, so any
  opcode (0..15), including AddComponent/MoveComponent/etc., deserializes successfully.
- A client can send packet 21 with a valid non-Cursor opcode (e.g. 2=AddComponent);
  the switch throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException. This is NOT NetStructReadException.
- LidgrenServerPeer.HandleDataMessage (LidgrenServerPeer.cs:282-306) wraps the
  OnMessageReceived callback (== ReadDataMessage) in try/catch(NetStructReadException)
  ONLY. ArgumentOutOfRangeException escapes -> unhandled exception in the message handler
  (process/log crash risk). Strong malformed-packet vector. (Client normally never sends
  non-Cursor opcodes directly - all mutations go through entity events.)
New INFO-LEAK finding — REQUEST_BACKUP_INDICES no permission check (packet 26):
- GameServer.cs:1040-1052 SendBackupIndices reads client-supplied `string savePath` and calls
  SaveUtil.GetIndexData(savePath) with no permission check.
- GetIndexData (SaveUtil.cs:943-1010) takes Path.GetDirectoryName + GetFileNameWithoutExtension
  then Directory.GetFiles(path, $".{baseName}.save.bk*") and parses each matched backup XML
  header (save time/current location/location type/next level type).
- => any client can probe arbitrary readable directories on the server filesystem and
  receive backup-index metadata (path traversal / metadata enumeration). Not full save
  content, but a real missing-permission info leak (same class as FileSender HIT #11).
CircuitBox entity-event path (server, 120-320) is builder-gated:
  helper requires !IsLocked() && item.CanClientAccess(client) && ClientHasRequiredItems.
  (Already noted.)
GameServer dispatcher notes:
- CAMPAIGN_SETUP_INFO new/load: both gated AllowedToManageCampaign(ManageRound).
- VOICE: checks SessionId == id + VoiceChatEnabled + !Muted.
- FILE_REQUEST: gated AllowFileTransfers (then FileSender HIT #11).
- ERROR handler: catches own parse errors, kicks on malformed data; MISSING_ENTITY
  subName length capped at 5; safe.
- REQUEST_BACKUP_INDICES: only missing-permission case found (above).
MARKED: GameServer 824-1105; SaveUtil 943-1010; CircuitBox server 38-70;
CircuitBoxNetStructs 24-55; INetSerializableStruct 328-355; LidgrenServerPeer 262-295.
[WAVE 27 — SegmentTableReader bounds]
SegmentTableReader.Read + SegmentReadMsg (Shared/Utils/SegmentTable.cs:145-330):
- Each segment is wrapped in SegmentReadMsg with lengthBits = nextPointer - pointer.
  The constructor throws a generic `Exception("Segment table is corrupt...")` if
  offset+lengthBits >= underlyingMsg.LengthBits, i.e. malformed segment pointers from a
  crafted UPDATE_INGAME/UPDATE_LOBBY cause an uncaught generic exception in ClientReadIngame.
  (ReadDataMessage is only wrapped by try/catch(NetStructReadException), which does NOT match.)
- Check() guards BitPosition > lengthBits AFTER each primitive read, so over-reading within a
  segment is detected (good), but MsgReader.ReadBytes still allocates `new byte[n]` BEFORE
  the Check -> the ServerEntityEventManager msgLength allocation/OOM vector is not mitigated.
- No privilege issue found, but corrupt segment-table pointers add another
  uncaught-exception/crash surface in the message handler.
MARKED: SegmentTable.cs 145-330.
[WAVE 28 — Voip/Event/Ready/FileSender/Voting + campaign store]
VoipServer.Read (server/Voip/VoipServer.cs:132-146):
- Reads directly into client.VoipQueue.Read(inc, discardData:false). No permission or length gate.
- VoipQueue.Read (Shared/Voip/VoipQueue.cs:136-169):
  * firstRead/more-recent path: reads 8 buffer lengths (0..255) then ReadBytes(each) — 8 arrays of up to 255 B.
  * MsgReader.ReadBytes (Message.cs:427-433) does `new byte[n]` + NetBitWriter.ReadBytes which does
    Buffer.BlockCopy(fromBuffer, readPtr, dest, ..., n) with NO bounds check; short VOICE packet ->
    ArgumentOutOfRange/ArgumentException outside the only NetStructReadException catch.
  * discard path: `msg.BitPosition += len*8` silently skips bits past the buffer end, no Check().
  * Conclusion: VOICE is a malformed-packet crash/amplification surface (~2KB alloc/read per packet).

EventManager.ServerRead (server/Events/EventManager.cs:28-102) — NEW CRASH VECTOR #3:
- EVENTMANAGER_RESPONSE dispatcher (GameServer.cs:1027) has NO permission/context gate.
- Client sends actionId(UInt16) + selectedOption(byte). When an active ScriptedEvent ConversationAction
  matches actionId and sender is a target client:
    if isIgnore=false and Options.Any() and !GetEndingOptions().Contains(selectedOption):
         var option = convAction.Options[selectedOption];  //  ^^^ NO Options.Count check
- selectedOption is a raw byte 0..255. Conversations normally have few options, so
  selectedOption >= Options.Count -> ArgumentOutOfRangeException escapes message handler.
- Also no per-client option constraint to the actual prompt.
ReadyCheck.ServerRead (server/GameSession/ReadyCheck.cs:89-108) — LOW annoy vector:
- Any client can send ReadyCheckState.Start when ActiveReadyCheck==null and instantly start a 30s ready
  check (`StartReadyCheck(client.Name, client)`), no permission, no cooldown. Only harmless if Start.

Client.cs (server/Networking/Client.cs:1-362) — SAFE for network reads:
- No client-controlled packet reader; TryTakeOverBot checks ironman/permadeath/PermanentlyDead/IsBot.
FileSender.ReadFileRequest (server/.../FileTransfer/FileSender.cs:336-436) — CONFIRMS #11 (client-side) + ack abuse:
- Dispatcher wraps with `if (ServerSettings.AllowFileTransfers)` (GameServer.cs:1021). Default is FALSE.
- When enabled: ANY client can send FileTransferType.CampaignSave and receive the FULL campaign save
  file (character/wallet/map data) — no campaign permission. Only gated by AllowFileTransfers.
  This is the campaign-save exfil variant of HIT #11.
- Submarine request only matches SavedSubmarines; Mod request requires AllowModDownloads + ModSender.Ready.
- Data-ack path: client can set `expecting` very high -> transfer jumps to Finished early (sender-side
  cancel), no server memory/privilege issue. StartTransfer caps: MaxTransferCount=16, 5/recipient.
Voting.cs (server/Networking/Voting.cs) — CONFIRMS #2 (full read of ServerRead+Finish):
- VoteType.TransferMoney + startVote=true (lines 319-333): reads amount(Int32), fromClientId(byte),
  toClientId(byte). NO MaximumMoneyTransferRequest cap, NO requirement that To==sender, NO requirement
  that From is sender-owned, NO money>=0 check, directly `pendingVotes.Enqueue(new TransferVote(...))`.
- TransferVote.Finish (81-97): fromWallet=From==null?Bank:From.Character.Wallet; toWallet likewise.
  Can transfer from ANY client's wallet (or bank) to ANY client's own wallet once vote passes.
- Vote.Update (206-232): if inGameClients.Count()==1 -> passed immediately. So on a server with the
  starter as the only in-game client, an uncapped bank -> self transfer can complete instantly.
- cooldown only via ShouldRejectVote (registered only on a FAILED vote), so a successful transfer can be
  repeated endlessly. This is the strongest confirmed money exploit (uncapped arbitrary transfer vote).
MultiPlayerCampaign.ServerRead (server/.../MultiPlayerCampaign.cs:835-1158):
- No top-level ManageCampaign check; relies on per-op gates:
  * repairs/hull/lost shuttle: GetBalance(client)/TryPurchase(client) -> client wallet (ManageWallets
    holders may also spend bank). No free service.
  * buy/sell: CargoManager.PurchaseItems/SellItems + AllowedToManageCampaign(SellSub/SellInventory).
  * upgrades/swaps: UpgradeManager.TryPurchaseUpgrade/PurchaseItemSwap gate HasPermissionToManageUpgrades
    (=ManageCampaign) + TryPurchase money.
  * Map.SelectLocation/SelectMission: ManageMap only. `currentLocIndex` SetLocation only if
    Map.AllowDebugTeleport (debug).
- UpgradeManager (Shared/.../UpgradeManager.cs:172-404): TryPurchaseUpgrade/PurchaseItemSwap both require
  ManageCampaign + TryPurchase; PurchaseItemSwap uses `isNetworkMessage` flag to disable charge — that
  path is server->client only, client cannot set it (ReadSoldItems/PurchasedItems never set it).
- CargoManager.SellItems (server/GameSession/CargoManager.cs:28-66): validates store.Balance, sub-item
  matching by actual entity, entity removal; the earlier "phantom sell" is constrained by
  `sellableItemsInSub` failsafe + ID binding, but Character-origin sold items are trusted as owned by
  the client when they have SellInventoryItems permission.

[WAVE 29 — peer layer + correction of severity + more server files]
**IMPORTANT CORRECTION (Waves 26-28): NO PROCESS CRASH — only message-batch abort/DoS.**
- LidgrenServerPeer.Update (LidgrenServerPeer.cs:118-144) wraps the whole incoming-message foreach in
  `try { ... HandleDataMessage(...) } catch (Exception e) { record GA error; if(Verbose) log/throw }`.
  The inner catch at 285 only catches NetStructReadException, but ANY other exception (e.g.
  ArgumentOutOfRangeException from ReadCircuitBoxMessage, NullReferenceException from ConnectionPanel,
  KeyNotFoundException from Fabricator/EventManager, ArgumentException from VoipQueue.Read, Overflow
  from ServerEntityEventManager msgLength) propagates to the OUTER catch at line 135.
- P2PServerPeer.Update (P2PServerPeer.cs:74-113) catches all exceptions similarly.
- Net effect: one malformed packet aborts processing of the REMAINING messages in the current batch
  (all other queued data for that server tick is dropped), records an analytics error, and the server
  keeps running. This is a real DoS/desync/availability vector but NOT a process crash. It also means
  a malicious client can repeatedly force the server to drop large batches of legit messages.
**ServerPeer.UpdatePendingClient (ServerPeer.cs:258-280) — CONFIRMS max-players bypass race (analysis 3):**
- `if (connectedClients.Count >= serverSettings.MaxPlayers) RemovePendingClient(pendingClient, ServerFull);`
  -> NO `return`. If `pendingClient.InitializationStep == Success` the code still does:
     connectedClients.Add(...); pendingClients.Remove(...); callbacks.OnInitializationComplete(...).
  A client that races auth+content-order into Success can be promoted past MaxPlayers.
- Same IP/account duplicates are only checked in HandleConnection/AuthInfo; a second connection from the
  same endpoint can get its own PendingClient and reach Success -> duplicate active slot.
**LidgrenServerPeer.HandleDataMessage (220-310):**
- Pending/unauth clients sending non-init messages are removed (AuthenticationRequired). Good.
- Ban list re-checked after auth. Good.
- Outer try/catch confirms all handler exceptions are contained (see correction above).
**P2PServerPeer (1-423):** server owns only the ChildServerRelay pipe; auth is delegated to owner peer.
- HandleDataMessage reads remote init steps via ServerPeer.ReadConnectionInitializationStep, adds
  PendingClient on ConnectionStarted. Ban/Heartbeat/Disconnect handled. No extra privilege path.
- Send() refuses unauthenticated connections (only OwnerConnection or connected). Good.
**ServerSettings (server, full 1-637):**
- `ServerRead` (167-265) requires ManageSettings. Under an admin client, unknown net-property keys
  execute `incMsg.BitPosition += (int)(8*size)` (line 202) with NO bounds/cap check -> admin-only
  malformed-packet skip vector (can jump bit position past/negative before the next read).
- `ReadPerks` (133-165): if NO connected client has ManageSettings, any client can change perks
  (offline-admin fallback) -> operator note confirmed.
**CharacterNetworking (server, full 1-832):**
- `ServerReadInput` (189-267) requires c.Character==this. `inputCount` is a raw byte -> a client can
  send 255 and force 255 reads from a short message (malformed/DoS vector, caught by peer outer catch).
- memInput is capped to 60 entries; networkUpdateID over 500 difference resets.
- `ServerEventRead` (269-349): EventType clamped via ReadRangedInteger. Treatment/Status require
  controlling the character. Talent refund/update gated by CanManageTalents (self or ManageBotTalents).
- Write-side (350-832) is server->client only.
**DebugConsole (server, 2780-2832):**
- `ServerRead` -> `ExecuteClientCommand` requires ConsoleCommands permission + per-command
  `PermittedConsoleCommands` (2795, 2804) + rate limiter. Any exception in command handler is caught.
- `AssignOnClientRequestExecute` only attaches a client-side handler; it does NOT bypass the
  permission/permitted-command checks. The readycheck command (2712-2747) is NOT dangerous by itself.
MARKED: DoSProtection 1-237, P2PServerPeer 1-423, ServerPeer 1-412, LidgrenServerPeer 1-575,
DebugConsole 2780-2832 (partial), ServerSettings 1-637, RespawnManager 1-704, KarmaManager 1-634,
CharacterNetworking 1-352/1-832 (full), CircuitBox 1-393, Steering 1-135, ServerEntityEventManager 1-579.
[WAVE 30 — Item/ReadPropertyChange + campaign permission fallback + small server files]
**Item.ReadPropertyChange (Shared/Items/Item.cs:3855-4030) — CONFIRMED unclamped floats + NEW string[] OOM:**
- Flow: property looked up against GetInGameEditableProperties(ignoreConditions:true); access check is
  done server-side after reading the property identifier: `ic.AllowInGameEditing`, `ConditionallyEditable
  .IsEditable(this)` and `CanClientAccess(sender)` (or open circuit-box access).
- float/int/Vector2/3/4/Rectangle etc. are read RAW (`msg.ReadSingle`, `msg.ReadInt32`) and then
  `TrySetValue` with NO min/max clamp. A client with CanClientAccess to an item/useful component
  (e.g. PowerContainer capacity, Reactor power, Pump flow, Door stuck, Wifi, Terminal) can set
  NaN/float.MaxValue/negative values through PropertyChange. Strongest confirmed "modified client"
  gameplay vector.
- **string[] branch (3982-3992)**: `int arrayLength = msg.ReadInt32(); new string[arrayLength]` with
  NO cap and BEFORE the allowEditing check. A client targeting any item with a string[]-typed editable
  property can allocate huge arrays (or throw Overflow on negative) even without edit permission.
  CAUGHT by peer outer catch (message-batch abort), but is a clean allocation/DoS vector.
- Enum branch: Enum.ToObject with arbitrary int; invalid enum values are NOT rejected server-side
  (Enum.ToObject doesn't throw for non-flags) -> property can be set to undefined enum values.
**Item.ServerEventRead (server/Items/Item.cs:185-243) — NEW NULLREF vector:**
- EventType.ComponentState / InventoryState read a componentIndex/containerIndex (bounded range) and
  then call `.ServerEventRead(...)` directly on `components[componentIndex] as IClientSerializable`.
  If that component is NOT IClientSerializable (many ItemComponents are not) the cast is null ->
  NullReferenceException. Same for `as ItemContainer` in InventoryState. Reaches peer outer catch;
  aborts the rest of the message batch. Triggerable by any in-game client.
- Treatment: CanInteractWith + HealingCooldown + target check; Combine: CanInteractWith both. OK.
**CampaignMode.AnyOneAllowedToManageCampaign (Shared/GameSession/GameModes/CampaignMode.cs:197-230) —
CONFIRMS "anyone can manage when no admin alive":**
- If no connected client has the permission (or only 1 client), returns true -> ALL clients can manage
  money/campaign/sell. In-game, if a permission holder exists but is dead/not in game, also returns true.
- Combined with MultiPlayerCampaign.ServerRead having no top-level ManageCampaign gate, a regular player
  can use the bank, buy upgrades/items, sell sub items while the admin is dead or in the lobby. This is
  the actual mechanic behind the "Shop rep bypass / phantom sell" candidate: permission authorization is
  collective, not per-actor.

[WAVE 31 — GameServer dispatch + ReadTakeOverBot + Client.TryTakeOverBot + crew/event server files]
**CONFIRMED — TAKEOVERBOT reserve-bench respawn-shuttle bypass (non-permadeath char swap):**
- GameServer.ReadTakeOverBotMessage (GameServer.cs:1569-1634): after Ironman global check and the
  "hireable character" branch (which correctly gates on ReplaceCost<=0 or ManageMoney/ManageHires),
  the **reserve-bench / new-hire branch** is:
  `if (botInfo is { Character: null } && (IsNewHire || IsOnReserveBench)) { if (IsUsingRespawnShuttle())
  SpawnAndTakeOverBotInShuttle(...) else SpawnAndTakeOverBot(...) }`.
- `IsUsingRespawnShuttle()` (3305-3308) returns true whenever `ServerSettings.UseRespawnShuttle` is on or
  RespawnManager.UsingShuttle — **it does NOT require Permadeath**.
- `SpawnAndTakeOverBotInShuttle` (1679-1712) NEVER calls `Client.TryTakeOverBot`. It directly:
  `DiscardClientCharacterData(client)`, `client.CharacterInfo = botInfo`, grants rename,
  `SpectateOnly=false`, `WaitForNextRoundRespawn=false`, `SetClientCharacterData(client)`.
  It does NOT check `Client.CharacterInfo.PermanentlyDead`, `RespawnMode.Permadeath`, or
  `ServerSettings.AllowBotTakeoverOnPermadeath`.
- Exploit: on any non-Ironman server with UseRespawnShuttle=true (or a shuttle already in use), a client
  who is ALIVE (or dead in normal respawn mode) can send ClientPacketHeader.TAKEOVERBOT with a
  reserve-bench/new-hire bot's ID. The server silently discards the player's current character data and
  reassigns them to the bot on the next respawn — a free character swap / duplicate-crew-slot abuser that
  ignores `AllowBotTakeoverOnPermadeath` and Permadeath. Non-shuttle branch still calls TryTakeOverBot
  (Permadeath + PermanentlyDead required), so only the shuttle branch is bypassed.
- `Client.TryTakeOverBot` (Client.cs:300-350) itself DOES enforce Permadeath + PermanentlyDead + IsBot, and
  never checks `AllowBotTakeoverOnPermadeath` — that flag is only checked in the "active bot" branch of
  ReadTakeOverBotMessage (line 1624). So if a server sets AllowBotTakeoverOnPermadeath=false, the
  reserve-bench branch STILL allows takeover (regardless of shuttle) as long as player was permadead —
  setting is effectively only applied to already-spawned bot characters.
**RECONFIRMED — EVENTMANAGER_RESPONSE is ungated (GameServer.cs:1026-1028):**
`GameSession?.EventManager.ServerRead(inc, connectedClient)` has NO permission check. Any fully joined
client can send it at any time. Combined with `ConversationAction.Options[selectedOption]` (raw byte
0-255 index into a small list) that is a client-to-server index-OOB vector whenever an active conversation
event is running and the sender is one of `TargetClients`.
**SERVER_SETTINGS / SERVER_SETTINGS_PERKS dispatch (GameServer.cs:977-982)** reachable by any connected
client; the security relies entirely on ServerSettings.ServerRead/ReadPerks internal checks (already
covered — ManageSettings and "no ManageSettings holder" fallback).
**READY_CHECK (GameServer.cs:1008-1010)** — ReadyCheck.ServerRead is fully ungated; any client can start
or spam the ready-check state.
**Small server files (Wave 31) marked DONE:**
CrewManager(177), EventLog(22), EventLogAction(55), EventObjectiveAction(35), HighlightAction(23),
MissionAction(42), StatusEffectAction(27), ConversationAction(215), VoipServerDecoder(230),
Client.cs(362), GameServer.cs read segments 975-1060/1560-1712/3305-3308.
[WAVE 31b — VOIP malformed-packet DoS + file-transfer confirmation]
**NEW — VOIP read has NO per-buffer bounds check (VoipQueue.Read, Shared/Networking/Voip/VoipQueue.cs:136-168):**
- On the "newer packet" path, for each of BUFFER_COUNT=8 buffers:
  `bufferLengths[i] = msg.ReadByte(); buffers[i] = msg.ReadBytes(bufferLengths[i]);`
  Each length is a raw byte 0-255; up to 8*255 = 2040 bytes are read without checking against
  `msg.LengthBits`. `MsgReader.ReadBytes` (Message.cs:427-432) allocates `new byte[numberOfBytes]` and calls
  `NetBitWriter.ReadBytes(...)`, so a short VOICE packet causes an out-of-bounds exception.
- On the discard path it also does `msg.BitPosition += len * 8` (VoipQueue.cs:165) with no bounds check.
- Dispatch: GameServer `case VOICE -> VoipServer.Read(inc, connectedClient)` (VoipServer.cs:132-146).
  Reachable by ANY connected client at any time, high-frequency unreliable packet. Exception is caught by
  the peer-layer Update try/catch, so it aborts the rest of the message batch for that tick (DoS/desync,
  not crash). Repeated VOICE packets = repeated legit-message drops. Same class as Wave-29 corrected DoS.
**FILE_REQUEST (GameServer.cs:1020-1024) — exfiltration only when AllowFileTransfers=true:**
- Dispatcher gates all FileTransferType on `ServerSettings.AllowFileTransfers`. When enabled, any client
  can send `FileTransferType.CampaignSave` (FileSender.ReadFileRequest) and the server will read and send
  the whole current campaign save file (characters, money, map progress) — no separate permission.
  Submarine/Mods are restricted to server-side knowledge; Mod requires AllowModDownloads+Ready+hash.
- `FileSender.ReadFileRequest` Data-ack branch also lets a client set `KnownReceivedOffset` arbitrarily
  (no `offset<=length` check), forcing a transfer to complete early (cancel). Minor availability vector.
**ServletRequestBackupIndices**: SendBackupIndices (GameServer.cs:1041-1051) has NO permission/time gate —
`REQUEST_BACKUP_INDICES` is always handled (line 1032-1034). Any client can ask for the save index data
(`SaveUtil.GetIndexData(savePath)`, client-supplied path) which includes backup indices, then use the
server's own save-path heuristic. Read-only information leak.
MARKED DONE: VoipServer(148), VoipServerDecoder(230), VoipQueue(172), Message.cs 415-433.
[WAVE 32 — GameServer dispatch (1-1105) + SaveUtil path/recon]
**CONFIRMED — REQUEST_BACKUP_INDICES is an unprivileged filesystem recon/read vector:**
- `GameServer.SendBackupIndices` (GameServer.cs:1041-1051): called for any connected client, no
  permission/rate gate. Client sends a path string `savePath`; server calls
  `SaveUtil.GetIndexData(savePath)`.
- `SaveUtil.GetIndexData(fullPath)` (SaveUtil.cs:943-1022): takes `Path.GetDirectoryName(fullPath)` and
  `Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(fullPath)` verbatim, then `Directory.GetFiles(path,
  ".{baseName}{FullBackupExtension}*")` and parses every matching `.save.bk*` XML to extract
  save-time/location/level metadata. No confinement to the campaign/save folder and no allowlist; a client
  can point it at arbitrary server paths (including `../` or absolute paths) and use `*`/`?` in baseName to
  enumerate backup files. Failures only log warnings. Returns metadata of backup saves (not full contents),
  but that still leaks filenames, locations, level type and save timestamps from arbitrary server dirs.
- `CAMPAIGN_SETUP_INFO` (GameServer.cs:889-961): `saveName` ends up in `SaveUtil.CreateSavePath` →
  `ToolBox.RemoveInvalidFileNameChars(fileName)` then Path.Combine(fixed save folder, fileName). So new
  campaign saveName cannot traverse out of the save folder (slash stripped); only reachable when
  AllowedToManageCampaign(ManageRound) fallback authorizes any client.
**CONFIRMED — RESPONSE_CANCEL_STARTGAME is ungated (GameServer.cs:857-866):**
Any connected client can send it while `isRoundStartWarningActive` to cancel a round start for the whole
server (broadcasts CANCEL_STARTGAME and AbortStartGameIfWarningActive). No permission check; usable as a
round-start griefing/DoS by any player in the lobby.
**CONFIRMED — VOICE handler validates sessionId only (GameServer.cs:962-976):**
`byte id = inc.ReadByte(); if (SessionId != id) return;` then `VoipServer.Read` — no rate limit, no
per-packet bounds check. The malformed-length VOIP DoS (Wave-31b) is exactly at this line.
MARKED: GameServer.cs 1-1105; SaveUtil.cs 943-1022.
[WAVE 33 — ClientReadServerCommand permission-skip refinement + CircuitBox DoS]
**CONFIRMED (refined) — SERVER_COMMAND ManageRound in campaign SKIPS the generic permission check and can
START a round without any permission (GameServer.cs:1741-1748 + 1849-1880):**
- For `command == ManageRound && mpCampaign != null`, the code "does nothing" (explicitly skips
  `sender.HasPermission(command)`).
- In the ManageRound case with `end=false` and `continueCampaign=false`, the relevant branch is:
  `else if (!GameStarted && !initiatedStartGame) { TryStartGame(); }`.
  There is NO `AllowedToManageCampaign(sender, ManageRound)` gate on this branch. So ANY connected client
  in a campaign server can repeatedly send SERVER_COMMAND=ManageRound/end=false to force a round start,
  even when the server is configured to require more readiness/vote. Start-round griefing/denial of
  `StartWhenClientsReady`. (Only the "end round" and "continue/transition" branches are properly gated.)
- Also `command` is `(ClientPermissions)inc.ReadUInt16()`; non-enum bit values fail `HasPermission` for
  normal players, but `ManageRound`/`ManageCampaign` values are always accepted when a campaign exists.
**CONFIRMED — CIRCUITBOX packet DoS (GameServer.cs:1543-1557):**
`ReadCircuitBoxMessage` reads `NetCircuitBoxHeader` via `INetSerializableStruct.Read` (throws
NetStructReadException wrapped), but the `header.Opcode` switch is:
```
CircuitBoxOpcode.Cursor => ...,
_ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(...)
```
A regular client can send `ClientPacketHeader.CIRCUITBOX` with any opcode other than Cursor and cause an
unhandled `ArgumentOutOfRangeException` inside `ReadDataMessage`. There is no try/catch in this method;
the exception escapes to the peer-layer `Update` catch, which aborts the rest of the message batch for
that tick. Repeatable DoS/desync by any joined client.
**SelectMode OOB (gated)**: only perf holders; `SelectedModeIndex = inc.ReadUInt16()` then
`GameMain.NetLobbyScreen.GameModes[modeIndex]` can throw if index is out of range.
MARKED: GameServer.cs 1491-1719, 1720-1995.
[WAVE 33] — GameServer dispatch + low-permission round-start + CircuitBox DoS
- ClientReadServerCommand (GameServer.cs:1720-1995) confirmed. `[PRIORITY]` ManageRound in campaign with
  end=false + continueCampaign=false reaches `TryStartGame()` at line 1875 with NO permission re-check
  (the generic HasPermission was skipped for ManageRound+mpCampaign!=null at 1741-1744). So a regular
  client in a campaign server can start a round. ManageCampaign+mpCampaign call `mpCampaign.ServerRead`
  which internally still checks most sub-actions (need to verify).
- SelectMode (1945-1953): `GameMain.NetLobbyScreen.SelectedModeIndex = modeIndex` then `GameModes[modeIndex]`
  with raw UInt16 -> possible ArgumentOutOfRange if modeIndex >= count. Permission-gated, but `SelectMode`
  can be given to regular users in some permission presets, and the exception propagates (DoS for that
  tick).
- SelectSub (1920-1944): gated; sub hash validated; safe.
- ManagePermissions (1960-1991): gated on sender.HasPermission(ManagePermissions); targetClient.ReadPermissions reads
  raw UInt16 and assigns directly (target != sender && != OwnerConnection). A ManagePermissions holder can
  grant themselves absolute any flag on any non-owner (already an admin-ish power).
- CIRCUITBOX: ReadCircuitBoxMessage (GameServer.cs:1543-1557) uses `INetSerializableStruct.Read` (raw, not
  TryRead) and for any Opcode OTHER than Cursor throws `ArgumentOutOfRangeException`. Non-admin client can
  send ClientPacketHeader.CIRCUITBOX with a crafted opcode => unhandled exception in the incoming-message
  loop (aborts remaining queued messages for that tick). No permission gate. NetCircuitBoxHeader.Read is also
  raw-NetStructReadException -> `HandleDataMessage` may disconnect on struct errors. This is a DoS/desync
  vector usable by any connected client.
- RequestBackupIndices (1032-1034 + 1041-1051) confirmed: no gate; server accepts client `savePath` string,
  calls SaveUtil.GetIndexData -> reads (enumerates) directory & .save.bk* files at that path and returns
  BackupIndexData list. `savePath` is NOT restricted to the active campaign save path; a rogue client can
  probe arbitrary server paths (path/list info leak, reconstruct campaign seed / backup dir contents).
  SaveUtil.GetIndexData -> GetIndexData(fullPath) -> Path.GetDirectoryName(fullPath), GetFileNameWithoutExtension
  -> Directory.GetFiles(path, ".{baseName}.save.bk*"). Then TryGetBackupIndexFromFileName + ExtractGameSessionRootElement
  reads each matched file. Only catches some exceptions; a bad path returns empty list.
- FileSender.ReadFileRequest (GameServer 1020-1024) only dispatches when AllowFileTransfers true; client
  can request TransferType.CampaignSave (send the campaign save file) and Submarine/Mod (out of scope).
  `StartTransfer` caps 16 active transfers / 5 per recipient but has no per-client file-type allowlist => 
  campaign save exfil when AllowFileTransfers is enabled. Good (user's HIT #11).
- RESPONSE_CANCEL_STARTGAME (857-866): any client can cancel a round-start warning / auto-restart
  countdown for the whole server while it's active. No permission check. Minor grief vector.
- UPDATE_CHARACTERINFO / ReadClientNameChange (4361-4447): name change cooldown 30s, sanitized, validated
  against allowed chars, but client can set `characterDiscarded=true` which discards their own campaign
  character data even while playing (self-sabotage only).
- WriteClientIngame / WriteClientLobby / position-update loops (2121-2538): server->client; only server-side
  position building, no client-controlled writes of note.
- TryStartGame / InitiateStartGame (2550-3000): server-authoritative round start; no client-read found
  in 2540-3457.
MARKED: GameServer.cs 1-5010 (full), incl. 2121-5010.
[WAVE 34 — **MEDICAL CLINIC FREE-HEAL EXPLOIT** (the "FreeMedicalClinic")]
**CONFIRMED — client controls NetAffliction.Price; any client can heal ANY crew member for free:**
- MedicalClinic.ServerRead (server/MedicalClinic.cs:55-86) has NO permission gate, only a rate limiter
  (20 req/5s, punishment=announce).
- ADD_PENDING -> ProcessNewAddition (88-95): reads arbitrary `NetCrewMember` from the network with
  `INetSerializableStruct.Read<NetCrewMember>` (client-controlled `CharacterInfoID` and
  `ImmutableArray<NetAffliction>` where each `NetAffliction` carries client-controlled `Identifier,
  Strength, VitalityDecrease, Price` — see Shared/MedicalClinic.cs:57-116).
- `InsertPendingCrewMember` (264-272) stores it verbatim; No server-side recomputation of Price.
- HEAL_PENDING -> ProcessHealing -> `HealAllPending(client)` (Shared/MedicalClinic.cs:193-217):
  `totalCost = GetTotalCost()` = **sum of the client-supplied `affliction.Price`** (line 320).
  `campaign.TryPurchase(client, totalCost)`:
    `CampaignMode.TryPurchase` (Shared/CampaignMode.cs:276-279) = `price == 0 || GetWallet(client).TryDeduct(price)`.
  Then for each pending entry it matches `crewMember.FindCharacterInfo(GetCrewCharacters())` and calls
  `health.ReduceAfflictionOnAllLimbs(affliction.Identifier, affliction.Prefab?.MaxStrength ?? affliction.Strength)`.
- **Exploit**: a normal client sends ADD_PENDING for any Team-1 CharacterInfoID (they can learn valid IDs via
  REQUEST_AFFLICTIONS / REQUEST_PENDING) with afflictions that mirror the target's real afflictions but with
  `Price=0` (or just any afflictions they want removed). Then sends HEAL_PENDING => totalCost 0 =>
  TryPurchase returns true => server removes those afflictions from the target character using the campaign
  bank / free. Cheap, no ManageMoney permission, no combat limitation (only IsOutpostInCombat() blocks,
  and only while friendly NPC is fighting crew).
- This is the **exact "FreeMedicalClinic" class**: server trusts network Price instead of deriving it from
  AfflictionPrefab (BaseHealCost + Strength*HealCostMultiplier) and applies healing to crew members whose
  CharacterInfoID the client supplied.
- Also REMOVE_PENDING uses client-supplied NetCrewMember/NetAffliction but only edits the pending list (no
  direct heal), so less impactful.
MARKED: server/MedicalClinic.cs 1-231, shared/MedicalClinic.cs 1-359, shared/CampaignMode.cs 245-300.
[WAVE 34b — MultiPlayerCampaign: TryPurchase/fallback + ServerReadCrew]
- `CampaignMode.TryPurchase(client, price)`: for NON-wallet managers it only draws from the client's own
  wallet (`wallet.TryDeduct(price)`). For wallet managers (or the no-manager fallback that makes everyone
  "allowed to manage wallets") it may supplement from the campaign bank:
  `balance + Bank.Balance` (MultiPlayerCampaign.cs:1507-1537). Consequence: on a server where no premium
  client has ManageMoney connected, normal players are treated as wallet managers and can spend bank money
  on upgrades/store items. Good supporting info for the campaign shop exploit.
- `MultiPlayerCampaign.TryPurchase` uses `GetWallet(client)` => `GetWallet(...)` for campaign returns the
  Bank for everyone (see Shared/CampaignMode.cs:271-274). So when the "anyone manages" fallback is active,
  client wallet == bank.
- **ServerReadCrew (1263-1434)**: `validateHires`/`updatePending` block (1376-1418) is gated by
  `location.HireManager != null && HasCampaignInteractionAvailable(sender, InteractionType.Crew)` only —
  NOT by ManageHires. A client standing near the crew/hire NPC can:
    * `validateHires`: call `TryHireCharacter(...)` for every pending hire (charges from their own wallet
      / bank via TryPurchase).
    * `updatePending`: move any hireable character into/out of the pending list, and to active service
      if below `MaxCrewSize` — again without ManageHires.
  `fireCharacter`/`renameCharacter` ARE gated by ManageHires (rename-self by RenamingEnabled only). So the
  un-gated component is the pending-hire edit + validation.
MARKED: MultiPlayerCampaign.cs 1177-1506, 1263-1434, 1507-1537.
[WAVE 34c — Campaign ServerRead spend paths + candidate #12 closed]
- MultiPlayerCampaign.ServerRead (835-1176): no top-level permission check; it is gated per section:
  * hull/item repair/lost-shuttle (908-939): gated by GetBalance(sender)+TryPurchase — a client at the
    outpost can buy services from the campaign wallet (or bank if wallet-manager fallback). Intended.
  * currentLocIndex teleport (941-944): only gated by `Map.AllowDebugTeleport` (server enum), not by client
    permission. On debug servers any client can force location changes.
  * selectedLocIndex/SelectMission (946-952): gated by AllowedToManageCampaign(ManageMap) — but fallback
    again makes it effectively open when no ManageMap holder.
  * buyCrate/purchased-items (954-1070): gated only by `HasCampaignInteractionAvailable(Store)`;
    `availableQuantity` clamps to stock, so no free duplication.
  * upgrades/item swaps (1127-1158): gated by HasCampaignInteractionAvailable(InteractionType.Upgrade),
    then `UpgradeManager.TryPurchaseUpgrade(...)/PurchaseItemSwap` — need to confirm those charge.
  * **sell path (1072-1124)**: filter by `SellSubItems`/`SellInventoryItems`, then CargoManager.SellItems.
    There is NO check in SellItems (server CargoManager.cs:28-66) that an Origin=Character item belongs to
    `client`; line 55 just does `Entity.FindEntityByID(item.ID) is Item entity` then removes it and adds
    `itemValue` to `campaign.GetWallet(client)`. So a client with SellInventoryItems can sell ANY item by ID
    (not just their own inventory), as long as store.Balance >= price. `GetSellValuesAtCurrentLocation`
    prices ANY non-null prefab (no "sellable" whitelist), so the main gate is the permission + store balance.
  * `BuyBackSoldItems` similarly has no ownership check, but it subtracts from store and adds to the buyer
    — a client can buy back the same sold item (self-duping store money) as the SoldItems list is
    client-influenced.
- **Candidate #12 CLOSED**: `ReadSoldItems` (Shared/MultiPlayerCampaign.cs:335-352) does
  `new SoldItem(ItemPrefab.Prefabs[prefabId], ...)`. `PrefabCollection<T>.this[Identifier]`
  (Shared/Prefabs/PrefabCollection.cs:266-279): missing key => `prefabs[identifier]` throws
  **KeyNotFoundException**; overridden prefab => `IndexOutOfRangeException`. So a client sending an unknown
  `prefabId` in a campaign `ServerRead` (near a store, or any client when the no-manager fallback applies)
  causes an unhandled exception in the incoming-message loop (batch abort / DoS). This is the exact
  candidate #12 — now verified against the indexer.
MARKED: Shared/MultiPlayerCampaign.cs 320-356; server CargoManager.cs 1-88; Shared/CargoManager.cs 379-427;
Shared/Prefabs/PrefabCollection.cs 255-300.
[WAVE 35 — CharacterNetworking.ServerReadInput + ServerEventRead]
- ServerReadInput (CharacterNetworking.cs:189-267): `byte inputCount` read from client, then a loop reads
  up to inputCount input records. There is NO cap on `inputCount` before reading (only the store cap
  `i < 60` is applied AFTER reading each record). A short packet with inputCount=255 causes
  OOB ReadRangedInteger/ReadUInt16 -> exception -> peer-layer batch abort (DoS). Reaching it requires
  c.Character == this, so it's an in-game client.
- Input records: aim is a raw UInt16 -> normalized cursor angle; interact is a UInt16 entity ID resolved
  server-side through `CanInteractWith` in UpdateNetInput (128-159). No direct item manipulation from
  inputs.
- ServerEventRead (269-349):
  * Treatment / Status: only own character; Status lets an incapacitated player kill themselves (self).
  * UpdateTalents: gated by CanManageTalents (own character or ManageBotTalents); server validates each
    talent via `TalentTree.IsViableTalentForCharacter`; safe.
  * ConfirmTalentRefund: gated.
- ServerWritePosition/EventWrite are server->client only.
MARKED: CharacterNetworking.cs 1-420 (all client-input-reachable portions), 189-349 emphasized.
[WAVE 30 — ChatMessage.ServerRead order DoS + Inventory gating]
**CONFIRMED — ChatMessage.ServerRead unknown-order DoS (server/Networking/ChatMessage.cs:19-143):**
- A client can send a chat message with `ChatMessageType.Order` and an arbitrary
  `OrderIdentifier`. At line 50:
  `var orderPrefab = orderMessageInfo.OrderPrefab ?? OrderPrefab.Prefabs[orderMessageInfo.OrderIdentifier];`
  `OrderPrefab.Prefabs` is `PrefabCollection<OrderPrefab>` (Order.cs:41). The indexer throws
  `KeyNotFoundException`/`IndexOutOfRangeException` for unknown/overridden id (PrefabCollection.cs:266-279).
  No permission pre-check -> any joined client can spam malformed Order chat packets to force an
  exception inside `ClientReadLobby`/`ClientReadIngame` ChatMessage segment. Caught by peer outer catch
  (batch abort), so a strong repeatable DoS/desync vector.
- The order option index IS bounds-checked (line 53-55). Text is truncated to MaxLength and RichString
  `‖` stripped unless SpamImmunity. Order commands only applied if `c.Character` alive & can speak.
- `msg is ChatMessageType.Order` also validates `orderTargetCharacter/Entity`.
**Inventory.ServerEventRead (server/Items/Inventory.cs:1-298) — reasonable gating, no obvious asset
stealing bypass found:**
- `IsInventoryAccessible()` uses `sender.Character.CanAccessInventory(this)` (line 62) BEFORE the shuffle.
- `HandleRemovedItems` (113-167) only removes items that are `IsInteractable(sender.Character)` and
  denies removal if the item was previously present but the sender can't access it.
- `HandleAddedItems` (169-224) requires the item to have a Pickable component that isn't mid-pick, have
  `AllowedSlots`, be IsInteractable, and passes an explicit `CanClientAccess`/previous-inventory access
  check. Handcuff items use an even stricter `AllowBotsAndPets` check.
- So drag-and-drop inventory reordering is checked; a custom client can't just yank any world item.

[WAVE 36 — Voting.ServerRead TransferMoney (original, exact code verified)]
**CONFIRMED — Voting.ServerRead TransferMoney vote bypass (server/Voting.cs:242-383 + 149-204 + 59-98):**
- `voteType = (VoteType)voteTypeByte` — casting int->enum never throws, so the try/catch is irrelevant.
- For `startVote=true` + `VoteType.TransferMoney` (319-333):
    int amount = inc.ReadInt32(); int fromClientId = inc.ReadByte(); int toClientId = inc.ReadByte();
    `if (!ShouldRejectVote(...)) pendingVotes.Enqueue(new TransferVote(sender, clientBySessionId(from),
     amount, clientBySessionId(to)))`.
  `ShouldRejectVote` (181-196) ONLY checks the 1-minute rejected-vote cooldown for the same vote type. It
  does NOT check `ServerSettings.MaximumMoneyTransferRequest` (that cap is only applied in
  `MultiPlayerCampaign.ServerReadMoney`, Voting.cs path), does NOT check amount >= 0, and does NOT check
  that From/To are the self/legit.
- `Vote.Update` (206-232): finishes when `ActiveVote.Timer >= VoteTimeout || inGameClients.Count() == 1`.
  With one in-game client, `yes==no==0`, `total==0`, but `passed = (... ) || inGameClients.Count()==1`
  -> **true instantly**.
- `TransferVote.Finish` (81-97): `fromWallet = From == null ? Bank : From.Character?.Wallet`; if
  `fromWallet.TryDeduct(amount)`, `toWallet?.Give(amount)`. So `From=null` (e.g. invalid session id) =
  spends the **campaign bank**; `To=null` = bank.
- **Impact**: any player can start a TransferMoney vote to move any amount (up to the source balance) from
  the campaign bank to their own wallet (or between wallets) with no `MaximumMoneyTransferRequest` cap,
  and it passes instantly whenever there is only one in-game client (or when enough other clients vote yes).
  Repeatable once the 1-minute rejected-vote cooldown expires. This is the money-minting/transfer exploit.
- Also note SubmarineVote path (335-348) does check `CanPurchaseSub` and `StartSubmarineVote` doesn't cap
  price either, but the actual purchase in `TrySwitchSubmarine` uses `CanPurchaseSub`/wallet.
MARKED: server/Voting.cs 1-430 (network read + vote logic).
[WAVE 37 — ServerSettings perks fallback + progress snapshot]
**CONFIRMED — ServerSettings.ReadPerks permission fallback (server/ServerSettings.cs:133-165):**
`HasPermissionToChangePerks` returns true if the sender has ManageSettings OR **no other connected client
has ManageSettings**. So on a server whose admin hasn't connected (or in PvP with no same-team manager),
any client can send SERVER_SETTINGS_PERKS and set the server's perk allowances. `ServerRead` itself is
hard-gated on ManageSettings (line 169), so the perk path is the looser one.
**Progress snapshot (research/ranges.json):** current coverage is 72 files fully read / 116 touched /
