  Server-side verdicts for every one of these already logged in waves 19-73; this is the CSHUB send map.
Key confirmations this wave:
  - ClientEntityEventManager.cs (1-297 FULL): CreateEvent() only checks ValidateEntity (removed/ID-freed)
    - NO ownership check client-side; a mod can queue an event for ANY IClientSerializable entity, the
      server-side CanClientAccess re-check is the only gate (verified waves 61-62). Write() retransmits
      pending events every 0.2s until server acks (LastSentEntityEventID). Read(): per-event bounds
      pre-check (BitPosition+16+8 > LengthBits), BitPosMismatch guard throws EntityEventException;
      entity-not-found -> ReportError(MISSING_ENTITY) + abort read (hostile server can spam client
      error telemetry / abort batches).
  - Camera.cs ClientWrite (175-183, marked): the SpectatingPos segment (HIT #8) is written ONLY while
    dead/spectating (`Character.Controlled == null || Controlled.IsDead`) and carries TWO RAW FLOATS.
    Server accepts it from anyone without a dead/spectating check (Wave 19) => crafted packet works for
    LIVE players too; and a CSHUB mod can just move the local camera while dead to aim server-side
    entity streaming anywhere (wallhack while dead, zero packets needed).
  - SendIngameUpdate (2540-2600): SyncIds includes client-written campaign.LastSaveID + per-NetFlags
    update IDs + CampaignCharacterDiscarded bit (self-sabotage, Wave 33) and a DEBUG flag
    `SpoofEntityManagerReceivedId` (2549) that makes the client claim LastReceivedID=1 -> server
    re-streams everything (desync-testing switch compiled into release).
  - Client chat write (client ChatMessage.cs 1-200 FULL + OrderChatMessage.cs 1-16): the client sends
    ONLY {ushort NetStateID, ranged type, ranged chatMode, string Text} - the sender name, sender
    character, radio targets are all server-derived. Orders go through the shared WriteOrder
    (priority 0..255 unclamped + malformed-id vectors, Wave 20). Client reads of hostile-server chat:
    ChatMessageType.ServerLog makes the CLIENT write lines into its own ServerLog file
    (GameMain.Client.ServerSettings.ServerLog.WriteLine) - local log injection / disk growth, bounded
    by LinesPerFile; ServerMessageBox types create client GUI boxes from server text (by design).
  - EntitySpawner.cs client (1-81 FULL): spawn/remove events are server->client only; removal path
    null-safe, spawn path = Item.ReadSpawnData/Character.ReadSpawnData (verified Wave 71).
  - Kick/Ban/Unban writers (3025-3080): SERVER_COMMAND + permission ushort + args; BanList client writes
    uint uniqueId in the admin ban-removal path (server verdict Wave 68). Server re-checks all perms.
  - KarmaManager client (148) / RespawnManager client / NetStats: display-only, no protocol writes.

[WAVE 76 - client peers (connect/auth) + client ServerSettings + voip writer]
ClientPeer.cs (1-255 FULL): the client-side initialization state machine.
  - Flow: AuthInfoAndVersion -> client sends ClientAuthTicketAndVersionPacket {Name, OwnerKey, AccountId,
    AuthTicket, GameVersion, Language} -> ContentPackageOrder -> client reads ServerPeerContentPackageOrderPacket
    and replies with an EMPTY struct (just the header, no package list!) -> this is the client half of the
    server-side HIT #10 join-without-mods (Wave 59): the protocol simply has no client package ack payload.
  - Password step: server sends salt + retriesLeft; client answers with
    SaltPassword(UTF8(password), salt) (ServerSettings.cs:1288 = interleave salt bytes + SHA1) - the
    password itself never crosses the wire. `AutomaticallyAttemptedPassword` is auto-sent when set.
Client "permissions" are UX-only: ClientAdminWrite() checks GameMain.Client.HasPermission(ManageSettings)
  locally before sending SERVER_SETTINGS - a mod bypasses it trivially; the real gate is the server-side
  ServerRead ManageSettings check (waves 32-33). Same for HasPermissionToChangePerks() - the client
  mirrors the server's ReadPerks fallback (wave 37/39) purely for UI.
Client ServerSettings.cs (126-315): write formats documented:
  - SERVER_SETTINGS: flags byte + [ExtraCargo + changed-properties dict (uint count + uint key + value)
    + monster toggles + BanList.ClientAdminWrite (uint uniqueId removals)] + [HiddenSubs] +
    [Misc: added/removed mission ids + traitorDangerLevel+1 byte] + [LevelSeed string].
  - SERVER_SETTINGS_PERKS: WritePerks payload.
  - ClientRead/ClientAdminRead (server->client): unknown-property skip `BitPosition += 8*size` - same
    unvalidated-skip family the server has (Wave 68), hostile-server only.
LidgrenClientPeer.cs (1-304 FULL): endpoint-mismatch drop (anti off-path spoof), disconnect string ->
  PeerDisconnectPacket.FromLidgrenStringRepresentation, Send() = PrepareForSending (optional compression)
  + PeerPacketMessage. DEBUG-only latency simulation. No trust surface beyond the server's own data.
P2PClientPeer.cs (1-435 FULL): same flow over Steam/EOS sockets; buffers init messages while not Success;
  heartbeat every second; timeout in-game/not-in-game; uses MessageFragmenter/Defragmenter - the client
  half of Wave 50: a HOSTILE P2P SERVER can exhaust CLIENT memory via unbounded FragmentCount/MessageId
  (client-side mirror of the server finding). OnP2PData drops packets from non-server endpoints.
VoipClient (70-95): the VOICE writer = header + QueueID byte + shared VoipQueue.Write (8 buffer lengths,
  verdict Wave 69 - server has no per-client voice rate limit beyond DoSProtection).
Net result: NO new client->server trust holes in the peer layer; everything is mirrored/gated server-side.
  Client-side hostile-server notes logged (defragmenter OOM mirror, settings skips, chat log injection W75).

[WAVE 83 - vitality formula + afflictions + StatusEffect.Apply core]

Affliction.cs: GetVitalityDecrease = 0 below ActivationThreshold; strength clamped [0, MaxStrength];
  per-Effect lerp(MinVitalityDecrease, MaxVitalityDecrease, strengthFactor); optional x MaxVitality
  (percent afflictions). RESISTANCE (line 449): _strength += StrengthChange x dt x (1 - resistance) -
  resistance only slows PROGRESSION, it does NOT reduce an already-applied strength. SetStrength does NOT
  clamp (clamped at read sites); NaN/inf rejected; _nonClampedStrength kept separately.

[WAVE 85 - armor pipeline in Limb.AddDamage + talent dispatch]
Limb.AddDamage (803-905): armor = DamageModifiers from limb params + from EVERY worn WearableSprite's
  WearableComponent. Per affliction: match by id/type; single unsynced roll -
  `random > affliction.Probability x damageModifier.ProbabilityMultiplier` => affliction BLOCKED entirely;
  SectorHit (directional armor sectors) decides which modifiers apply; finalDamageModifier = PRODUCT of
  matching DamageMultiplier values; DeflectProjectiles armor is lerped toward 1 by `penetration`
  (armor pen formula); MultiplyByMaxVitality afflictions scale by MaxVitality/100;
  talent hook OnAddDamageAffliction can REPLACE the affliction instance; AttackResult carries
  afflictions + applied modifiers. Modding: add a DamageModifier to a Wearable = directional armor.
CharacterTalent dispatch (80-120): talent = Dictionary<AbilityEffectType, List<CharacterAbilityGroupEffect>>;
  CheckTalent(type, abilityObject) runs all groups registered for that effect type;
  ActivateTalent(addingFirstTime) activates all groups (on-give effects) - this is the server-side
  framework behind AbilityFlags grants (Wave 3), stat boosts and periodic abilities; XML-driven via
  abilityeffecttype attr.

[WAVE 87 - order -> bot objective pipeline (impact of the priority-255 vector)]
CharacterInfo (587-613): MaxCurrentOrders = 3, HighestManualOrderPriority = MaxCurrentOrders = 3.
  GetManualOrderPriority computes 1..3 by AssignmentPriority ordering.
AIObjectiveManager (36-40, 800-820): bot objective priority scale:
  HighestOrderPriority = 70, LowestOrderPriority = 60;
  **GetOrderPriority = Lerp(60, 69, InverseLerp(1, 3, ManualPriority))** - InverseLerp clamps, so an
  injected ManualPriority of 255 maps to exactly the same 69 as 3!
  => CORRECTION of Wave 20: the unclamped 0..255 priority byte does NOT give higher-than-UI bot
  priority; it saturates. It can only pick priority BELOW normal (0 -> 60, the bottom slot). Real
  impact of the priority byte = demotion/misplacement, NOT super-priority. Downgrading the Wave 20
  claim "orders with priority up to 255 preempt normal bot orders" accordingly.
Character.SetOrder (4146-4235): hearing gate CanHearCharacter(orderGiver) unless force; AutoDismiss
  Operate orders auto-dismiss other operators of the same device; Movement orders replace each other;
  duplicate removal; OnGiveOrder talent hook; humanAI.SetOrder -> objective creation. CrewManager
  AddOrder is the shared entry (already FULL, Wave 66).

  (cooldown + threshold + CanStartEventSet: min distance from start/end, min mission time, intensity
  window, AllowAtStart), ResetTime sets re-enqueue on finish, child sets re-checked in a do/while.
  All server-authoritative; EVENTMANAGER_RESPONSE client input verdict unchanged (Wave 45/66).

[WAVE 89 - ScriptedEvent action machine + server-side conversation read]
ScriptedEvent.Update (387-453): linear action list with GoTo labels; currentAction.CanBeFinished() false
  => event Finish (defensive); IsFinished(ref goTo): goTo empty -> next action, else Reset all actions and
  jump to label (not found -> warning + Finish); index out of range -> Finish. Cached targets refresh on
  crew change / entity spawn / removal.
Server EventManager.ServerRead (28-102) - re-verified the Wave 45/66 OOB: `selectedOption = ReadByte()`,
  255 = Ignore (safe path with IgnoreClient/Retrigger), otherwise convAction.SelectedOption = raw byte and
  `convAction.Options[selectedOption]` is indexed ONLY inside the `!GetEndingOptions().Contains(selectedOption)`
  branch - no bounds check => IndexOutOfRange for selectedOption >= Options.Count. Confirmed unchanged.
  ForceSay/targets write-back are server-side.

[WAVE 94 - TraitorManager (server FULL)]
TraitorManager (server, 530 lines FULL): 
  - viability: TraitorsMinPlayerCount gate (DEBUG build ignores it and warns), client must have a live,
    not-incapacitated character and must not already be a traitor in an active event.
  - danger ladder: maxDangerLevel from ServerSettings.TraitorDangerLevel; successful previous event
    (with a still-viable traitor) promotes to RequiredPreviousDangerLevel+1; RequirePreviousDangerLevelCompleted
    prefabs are gated; suitable initial = RequiredCompletedTags.None + danger-eligible.
  - selection: GetRandomByWeight - events weighted by Commonness x (roundsSinceLastSelected/MaxHistory)^2
    (recently-used events exponentially suppressed); traitor weighted by (roundsSince/MaxHistory)^2 or 1.0.
  - secondary traitors: percentage of viable non-main clients (ceil) or fixed amount.
  - all Rand.RandSync.Unsynced - server-authoritative, fine (nothing client-side depends on it).
  Shared TraitorManager (48): TraitorResults struct only (votedAsTraitor + objective success flags).
  No client-writable surface: TRAITOR_MESSAGE is server->client only (client GameClient case 926 reads).

[WAVE 101 - Turret/Projectile/Structure network surfaces (send-side closure)]
Turret: NO ClientEventWrite anywhere (client partial + shared) - turret aiming goes through
  CharacterInput (aim) and Item use events; the only network surface is ServerEventWrite
  (projectileID + 16-bit clamped rotation) -> ClientEventRead applies targetRotation only when
  not the current user; launch handled by projectileID 0/65535 sentinels. VERDICT: no turret
  packet vector; the Wave 62 "no client-writable path" stands, now confirmed by full read.
Projectile (server partial FULL): ServerEventWrite only (launch bool + user id + raw launchPos
  X/Y + rot + spread counter + sub id; stuck bool). NO ServerEventRead -> clients CANNOT send
  projectile state; shooting is driven server-side from character inputs/Use. Clean.
Structure: ServerEventWrite (byte sectionCount + 8-bit normalized damage per section) ->
  client ClientEventRead (byte sectionCount; count mismatch = exception + analytics; per-section
  damage ranged 0..1 x MaxHealth). NO client->server structure events. The Wave 20 wallSectionIndex
  OOB is in OrderChatMessage: client writes the section index as RAW BYTE (cast from int?; 0 if
  null), server indexes Structure.Sections[index] on read => byte 0..255 vs typical sections count
  (<= 255?), small structures (door 1-2 sections) are the OOB target; still log-spam class, verdict
  unchanged. Client writes the byte via shared WriteOrder - a mod can send any value 0..255.

[WAVE 141 - HumanAI big tail: relocation/dragging/hull safety/team logic]

  oxygenFactor (lerp around HULL_SAFETY_THRESHOLD, 30-70% oxygen window) x waterFactor (visible-hull
  summed water volume! multi-floor rooms) x fireFactor x enemyFactor (in-hull enemies -90% each,
  adjacent visible -22.5%) x dangerousItemsFactor (any dangerous item in hull => 0); cached per
  knownHulls with staleness; static variants allow ignoring single factors; CalculateObjectiveHullSafety
  ignores pressure protection. Crew-wide helpers IsTrueForAll/AnyBotsInTheCrew.

[WAVE 142 - HumanAI report speech + corpse-drag reaction + RespondToAttack]
Report speech (1130-1190): first problem => order spoken; FriendlyNPC speaks with 60s dedup identifier
  (prefab+hull), player-team bots push the order to CrewManager AND send OrderChatMessage with
  HighestManualOrderPriority to the server (this is the Wave 87 pipeline origin - bots always use
  priority 3).
IsBallastFloraNoticeable (1192-1210): flora must have broken through AND >2 alive branches in the hull
  (first 2 branches at room edges are ignored).
Corpse-drag reaction (1230-1250): seeing a player drag a OUR-team corpse => security bots switch to
  Arrest mode, others Retreat; dialog by security/civilian tag, 10s dedup, one reaction at a time.
OnHealed/OnAttacked (1252-1300): heal amounts accumulate per healer (RespondToAttack subtracts them:
  realDamage = damage - healed); incapacitated => respond immediately + freezeAI (autonomous objectives
  reset); PLAYERS always respond immediately (their update loop doesn't run, otherwise other NPCs would
  ignore attacks on players); bot attacks accumulate affliction strengths per attacker before the
  scheduled response.
RespondToAttack (1300-1400+): totalDamage includes karma-weighted afflictions; zero real damage =>
  no reaction to karma-only hits (medics/poison) unless the applier is a player; incapacitated bots
  freeze AI and rebuild autonomous objectives; CPR damage is forgiven ("assume they are helping");
  accidental friendly damage (bot, not unstable, no combat action): <=minor threshold tolerated,
  else RETREAT from the clumsy teammate; deliberate friendly damage => inform other NPCs above
  minor threshold, DetermineCombatMode with special security dialog; isAttackerInfected noted
  (husk suspects); minor/major thresholds 10/40 (40+ for own-team).

[WAVE 143 - HumanAI witness/instigator/criminal system]

  (cultist husks); WITNESSING = sees victim's or attacker's hull OR sees attacker through WINDOWS;
  non-witnesses react only if victim not knocked down, same team, no orders, within ReportRange;
  non-security witnesses TRIGGER SECURITY bots instead of fighting.

  INSTIGATOR system: if attacker is friendly but FindInstigator() finds the real culprit, guards stand
  down (security => None, others => instigator's witness reaction or Retreat) - "guards don't react to
  player's aggressions when there's an instigator around"; FriendlyNPC attackers (not mentally
  unstable): security => GuardReaction or Offensive, others => WitnessReaction or Retreat;
  criminal history: attacker already targeted => cumulativeDamage x2 + AllowHoldFire off + IsCriminal/
  IsActingOffensively flags set; IsCriminal attackers always at least minor-threshold reaction;
  > majorDamageThreshold => IsCriminal+IsActingOffensively and security goes Offensive while others
  Defensive/Retreat.

[WAVE 148 - EnemyAI UpdateAttack deep dive: post-attack behaviors, ranged checks, circling]

    followThrough; with SecondaryCoolDown => wait timer, target-switch blocks attack, limb switch

  door attacking via path node ConnectedDoor (not traversable + no friendly access) => door target;
  pursuit margin = min(range*0.9, 300); manual steering same-hull+visible+swimming/pursue;

  invert dir -> speed rotation -> drop offset -> break (melee) or FallBack; strike = melee within
  Range*multiplier and facing lerp(0.5,0.9,aggressionIntensity); multiplier grows w/ target speed
  (cap 2); aggressionIntensity += AggressionCumulation per strike.
Straight ranged (2260-2295): advance >0.9x range, FallBack < min(250, range*0.25) + Reverse,
  wander+avoid outside when can't attack; avoidance weight 30.
Resolution (2310-2340): ranged => AimRangedAttack; UpdateLimbAttack failure => IgnoreTarget;

Homoglyphs: ~1850 lines of uint[] groups mapping lookalike Unicode chars to ASCII canonical (first element).
  Compare(a,b): exact match => true; LENGTH MUST EQUAL else false; per-char: equal or same homoglyph group.
  Normalize(input): maps every char through group => ASCII canonical. USED BY: client SpamServerFilter

  gamemode, DebugConsole server-side client lookup by name (kick/ban evasion via lookalike names => BLOCKED:
  console finds client by homoglyph-normalized compare - you cannot evade admin kick with Cyrillic 'а').
=> NO vectors; this is anti-abuse infrastructure. Note: Compare requires equal length => inserting zero-width
   chars evades Compare but Normalize catches groups only for listed chars (zero-width not in table - possible
   chat-filter bypass for custom filters, but client chat filtering happens server-side anyway).

[WAVE 197 - CONFIRMED FLAGSHIP: Talent Overspend (user find) + TalentOverspendModule.cs written]
USER FIND VERIFIED against closed files (CharacterNetworking server read, TalentTree, CharacterInfo, Character):
  Server EventType.UpdateTalents handler (BarotraumaServer/.../CharacterNetworking.cs:308-331):
  CanManageTalents (own char = always true) -> u16 count -> per talent u32 UintIdentifier ->
  TalentTree.IsViableTalentForCharacter(this, id, talentSelection) -> GiveTalent(id).
  THREE stacked flaws:
   1) budget gate = GetTotalTalentPoints() - selectedTalents.Count <= 0 where GetTotalTalentPoints()
      = level + AdditionalTalentPoints = TOTAL EARNED (GetAvailableTalentPoints() = correct remaining
      is NEVER used server-side).
   2) talentSelection is a fresh PACKET-LOCAL list: budget resets to full on every packet.
   3) GiveTalent performs no point check at all. Bonus: already-unlocked talents early-out true
      ("backwards compat") so re-sent unlocks re-validate free.
  Structural prereqs ALSO evaluated against the packet-local list => every packet must carry its own
  in-packet prefix chain (required-tree stages satisfied + earlier stages satisfied). Server SKIPS
  non-viable entries without consuming talentSelection => junk entries don't waste budget.
  Blocked-tree rule: any selected talent of a blocking (non-maxed) subtree kills the target in that
  packet => conflicting targets deferred across packets; blocker stops blocking once maxed.
  Confirmation channel = server echo: server GiveTalent emits UpdateTalentsEventData back; client
  ClientRead -> GiveTalent locally -> Info.UnlockedTalents grows (progress signal for the loop).
  Campaign persistence: UnlockedTalents serialized in CharacterInfo.Save => PERMANENT profit.
  Wire format client->server: WriteRangedInteger(EventType.UpdateTalents, 0, 18) + u16 count +
  u32 UintIdentifier * count (vanilla writes its whole characterTalents list = why vanilla client
  can't exploit: full-list budget starves new entries).
  MODULE: research/mods/TalentOverspendModule.cs - Harmony prefix on Character.ClientEventWrite
  (payload swap on queued UpdateTalents events), reflection mirror of internal TalentTree/TalentSubTree/
  TalentOption (stages/required/max/required-trees/blocked-trees), exact server replay simulator
  (budget + viability in vanilla order), packet builder (required-tree fills -> earlier-stage fills ->
  unconfirmed, blocker-conflict deferral), echo-based state machine w/ timeout + stall abort. Self-pump
  via Character.Update postfix. File also documents the full wire format + trust-chain analysis.

[WAVE 199 - TalentOverspendModule build-compat fix (user's workshop compile error CS1061)]
User compiled against the INSTALLED game binary (WorkshopMods/2880887340/CSharp) = a different build than
our repo source. Two compile-time hazards in the module fixed:
 1) Character.IsTalentLocked(Identifier) is public in THIS repo (Character.cs:5811, stat Locks via
    StatTypes.LockedTalents) but ABSENT in the user's game build => CS1061. Replaced with OPTIONAL
    reflection (_miIsTalentLocked, resolved in EnsureReflection; guarded call in StructurallyViable).
    Degradation is safe: the server-side IsViableTalentForCharacter enforces its own locked-talent
    check, so a missing local mirror can only make the simulator OVERESTIMATE viability; the packet
    builder's retry/stall logic absorbs that (packet sends, server skips, no progress, stall abort).
 2) TalentPrefab is INTERNAL in source (class TalentPrefab : PrefabWithUintIdentifier, static public
    field TalentPrefabs) => TalentPrefab.TalentPrefabs.Find would ALSO fail CS0122 from a mod assembly
    on this source layout. Replaced with a lazily-built Identifier->UintIdentifier map via reflection:
    enumerate (IEnumerable)TalentPrefabs, read Prefab.Identifier + PrefabWithUintIdentifier.UintIdentifier
    (both public base properties) => _uintById cache (ResolveUintIdentifiers). Never references the
    internal type at compile time.
LESSON (generalizes to all our modules): compile against the TARGET BINARY's public API, not the repo
source. Repo-fork may be newer/older than the installed game. Rule of thumb: internal-in-source types
(TalentPrefab, TalentTree, TalentSubTree, TalentOption...) and recently-added public members
(IsTalentLocked, EventManager additions) must go through reflection with graceful degradation.
Public-safe members used by the module (verified in source): Character.ClientEventWrite, nested public
UpdateTalentsEventData + EventType.MinValue/MaxValue, Info.GetTotalTalentPoints/GetUnlockedTalentsInTree/
UnlockedTalents, Job.Prefab.Identifier, GameMain.Client.CreateEntityEvent, DebugConsole.NewMessage.

[WAVE 205 - hotfix: AmbiguousMatchException crash on Start Flood]
PrefabCollection<T> has TWO TryGet overloads in source: TryGet(Identifier, out T) at :299 AND
TryGet(string, out T) at :319. Our GetMethod(name, flags, null, new[]{typeof(Identifier), typeof(T).MakeByRefType()}, null)
threw AmbiguousMatchException at runtime (binder still considers both candidates on this build) —
crashed the WHOLE CLIENT from the GUI click handler (no try/catch in OnClick => GameMain.Update died).
Fix: replaced both remaining exact-types GetMethod calls (TryGet + IsTalentLocked) with explicit
parameter loops (name match + param count + param type + IsByRef check). No GetMethod-with-types
calls remain in the module — all reflection now ambiguity-proof.
LESSON: never use GetMethod(name, ..., types, ...) against target-binary internal APIs; always
enumerate GetMethods() and filter manually. ALSO: GUI OnClick handlers run inside GameMain.Update —
an unhandled exception there kills the client. All public entry points should get try/catch wrappers.

[WAVE 207 - hotfix: "TalentPrefab reflection unavailable" — field-vs-property divergence]

DIVERGE on member kinds:
  Prefab.Identifier:      repo = property? binary = PUBLIC READONLY FIELD (Prefab.cs:106)
  PrefabWithUintIdentifier.UintIdentifier: property in both ({ get; set; })
  TalentOption.RequiredTalents/MaxChosenTalents: public readonly FIELDS in binary (:125,:128) — our
    GetField already correct

  TalentSubTree.Identifier: PROPERTY ({ get; }) — GetProperty correct
  TalentTree.TalentSubTrees: readonly FIELD — GetField correct
Only the TalentPrefab identifier mapping was broken: code did GetProperty("Identifier") on a field.
Fix: new GetMemberByName(type,name) helper (FieldInfo ?? PropertyInfo) + ReadMember switch used for
the UintIdentifier map; base-chain walk now tries both kinds per level. _piPrefabId/_piUintId types
changed PropertyInfo -> MemberInfo.
META-LESSON (biggest of the whole session): the repo source is NOT the shipped binary. For every
reflected member, resolve field-or-property dynamically; never trust repo declarations for member
kind. The dnSpy dump /tmp/BarotraumaMod is the ground truth for the user's build.

[WAVE 208 - TalentOverspend v2: "unlock everything" + crash fix. LIVE-CONFIRMED EXPLOIT]
User field report: EXPLOIT WORKS (packets granted talents at 1 point; more levels -> tiers 1-2 unlocked).
Two bugs fixed + behavior upgrade:
 1) STALL ROOT CAUSE: plan included ALL talents per stage, but server caps grants at MaxChosenTalents
    per stage => unreachable leftovers kept "unconfirmed" non-empty forever => never DONE, stall-abort.
    FIX: BuildPlan now takes only (Max[stage] - alreadyUnlocked) talents per stage => plan empties => DONE.
 2) CRASH 'crewlayabout' KeyNotFoundException in BuildPacket: SelfTest created an ENGINEER test char and
    BuildTreeMirror("engineer") CLOBBERED _subs/_talentSub with engineer data while _plan still held the
    player's job talents. Next Start: _plan!=null && _planJobId matched => no rebuild => player talent
    looked up in engineer dictionary. FIX: (a) _mirrorJobId tracked; Update() rebuilds mirror+plan when
    _mirrorJobId != player job; (b) SelfTest.Run() finally-block rebuilds mirror for controlled char;
    (c) BuildPacket uses ContainsKey defensively.
 3) "UNLOCK EVERYTHING" strategy: blocker subtrees only block while NOT fully maxed (vanilla rule:
    HasAnyTalent && !HasMaxTalents). So: plan orders subtrees by BlockedTrees-count DESC (max blockers
    first); BuildPacket processes ONE active subtree per packet (first with unconfirmed), filling its
    required-tree stages + own earlier stages in-packet, then its new talents. Blockers get maxed
    before their victims start => the whole tree cascades open. Removed the old per-packet
    excluded/targets resolution (single-active makes it moot).
 4) SendInterval 0.25 -> 0.15s for near-instant clears after level-ups.
Workflow for the user: Start Flood once; on level-up just press Start again (or keep it armed — plan
rebuilds only on job/mirror change but unconfirmed recalcs each packet, so it auto-continues). DONE
message when the reachable maximum (max-per-stage of every subtree) is unlocked.

[WAVE 209 - THE missing piece: blockers need FULL MAX, not Required (binary-verified)]
User field test: base rows 1-2 unlocked, chosen specialization opened, but OTHER specializations
never unlocked + stall "unlocks nothing". Root cause in shipped binary TalentTree.cs:
  TalentTreeMeetsRequirements = requiredTrees.All(HasEnoughTalents) && !blockedTrees.Any(HasAnyTalent && !HasMaxTalents)
  HasMaxTalents(subtree) = ALL stages at MaxChosenTalents.
So a blocker stops blocking only when EVERY stage of it is filled to Max (>= MaxChosenTalents),
NOT RequiredTalents. My FillStage filled to Required => chosen spec stayed "any selected && !max"
=> permanently blocked all other specializations => stall.
FIX: FillStage now fills to s.Max[stage]. Everything cascades: blocker-first plan + max-fill =>
blocker fully maxed in its packet(s) => victims unlock in their packets => whole tree. Max-fill also
trivially satisfies HasEnoughTalents for "requires". Combined with plan-budget=Max (wave 208), the
entire job tree is now reachable regardless of real points — the whole point of the exploit.
Also explains user's "6th skill": stage Max caps are real server caps; extra points roll over to
next stages/subtrees per plan order — correct behavior, not a bug.

[WAVE 210 - hotfix: regression from wave 209 Max-fill — support talents burned the budget]
Wave 209 changed FillStage to fill to Max. With budget 1 and multi-stage trees, FillStage's
"pass 2: confirmed members as pure support" added ALREADY-UNLOCKED talents into the packet to pad
the stage to Max. Result: expectedNew=0 always, instant stall (packets 0). Deeper: it wasn't just
cosmetic — the SERVER counts re-sent unlocked talents against the packet-local budget
(selectedTalents.Count) even though GiveTalent no-ops, so a single support talent ahead of the real
new talent ate the whole 1-point budget. Also broke 3/5-point runs once supports appeared.
FIX: FillStage sends ONLY not-yet-unlocked members. Unlocked prerequisites are satisfied by the
server's own early-out (GetUnlockedTalentsInTree().Contains(talent) => viable=true) WITHOUT being
in the packet — no re-sending needed, no budget burn.
Note: stage "needs" within one packet are best-effort padding; if fewer unconfirmed exist than
Max, packet is shorter — server-side HasEnough/HasMax evaluate against packet selections UNION
already-unlocked, so viability still holds for what's sendable.

[WAVE 211 - hotfix: CS0103 'have' — my wave-210 string replace dropped the declaration line]
When replacing FillStage body via python string replace, the new body omitted "int have = acc.Count(...)"

and re-print the full method after every edit for eyeball verification.

[WAVE 212 - v3: Required-supports fix + full talent dumps (user request)]
User field report v2: base rows unlock (4+4 with 16 pts), specializations NEVER unlock, stall.
ROOT CAUSE FOUND in binary IsViableTalentForCharacter: the required/blocking subtree checks
(HasEnoughTalents/HasAnyTalent/HasMaxTalents) evaluate against the PACKET-LOCAL selectedTalents ONLY.
There is NO early-out for required trees based on already-unlocked talents (the early-out applies
only when the TALENT ITSELF is already unlocked). => a specialization talent needs its required-tree
stages satisfied IN THE SAME PACKET, i.e. base talents MUST be re-sent as supports every packet.
But re-sent unlocked talents burn packet budget (wave 210) => Supports must be minimal: exactly
RequiredTalents per required stage, not Max.
FIX: FillStage split into (acc,s,stage,unlocked,toMax). Required-fills for supports (packet prereqs
+ tier prereqs); Max-fill only relevant for the ACTIVE blocker tree, which the plan already handles
by budgeting its own stages to Max (v2) — its own unconfirmed talents ARE the max-fill.
Also removed both direct TalentPrefab.TalentPrefabs.Find lookups (CS0122 hazard on shipped binary —
TalentPrefab is internal): new reflection-safe ResolveNames() + TalentLabel(id) via DisplayName/Name.
BUDGET MATH (important, documented): packet cost = supports + new grants must be < GetTotalTalentPoints()+1.
With 16 pts: 4 supports (2 rows x req 2) + 12 new = fits. With 1 pt: only 1 grant/packet and spec
talent needs supports first => spec unreachable at 1 pt in one packet; BUT across packets with
2+ pts: [sup1..supK, newT] needs total > K. GiveXP to 5-6 before flooding specializations if it
stalls — or re-run repeatedly: each packet re-attempts with full budget.
DEBUG DUMPS (user request): (1) on arm: full TREE MAP — every subtree (requires/blocks), every
stage with req/max, every talent id + display name + wire uint; (2) per packet: every entry with
id, name, GRANT/SUPPORT/declined state. All via reflection-safe TalentLabel.

[WAVE 213 - v4 FINAL: wire-order bug found via tree map (user dump cracked it)]
User's dumps exposed THE ordering bug: packet contents showed grayshirt talents FIRST, primary
supports AFTER. Cause: "ordered" re-sorted the packet by _plan sequence, and plan is
blockers-first => blocker talents preceded their required-tree supports in wire order =>
required-subtree check evaluated against an EMPTY selection => everything declined.
Vanilla semantics: HasEnoughTalents/HasAnyTalent evaluate against packet-local sel; prereqs must
PHYSICALLY PRECEDE dependents in the byte stream.
FIX A: wire order = packet CONSTRUCTION order (supports -> tier prereqs -> new talents).
  No re-sorting.
FIX B: BuildPlan rewritten as proper dependency pass (Kahn-like): a subtree enters the plan only
  after all its requires are done; among ready ones, most-blocks first. Assistant example: primary
  => grayshirt => apprentice => clown. Guarantees supports exist (unlocked) by the time a
  dependent tree's packet is built, and within a packet for fresh chars (FillStage in-packet).
Expected assistant flow (fresh, 3+ pts): P1: [primary stage0 x2, stage1 x2, ...up to budget]
  then P-next: grayshirt [waitinglist, nonthreatening/tastytarget, appleaday/insurance, friends],
  then apprentice, then clown. Each tree fully maxed (all stages to Max) before next starts.
Note: primary stage Max=6 each => with small budgets primary takes several packets; spec trees
  (Max=1/stage) take 4-5 talents each => one or two packets. DONE when plan empties.

[WAVE 214 - v5: THE stage-gating fix — unlocked supports must be RE-SENT]
User logs (1pt run: 6 packets, 6 grants, stage1 never reached) proved it: vanilla stage-gating
(optionStageCompleted = stage.HasEnoughTalents(sel)) and required-subtree checks evaluate ONLY
against the packet selection. Wave-210 "never resend unlocked" made stage1+ permanently declined
(empty stage0 count in packet => "break" out of the subtree's stage loop).
FIX: FillStage Required-mode re-includes ALREADY-UNLOCKED members, placed FIRST (they're
guaranteed viable via the unlocked early-out, anchoring the stage count), then new members.
Each support burns one budget slot (talentSelection.Add even when GiveTalent no-ops) =>
minimal Required-fill only; per-packet log now shows "supports re-sent: N (budget floor ≈ N+1)".
Budget floors: primary stage1 from fresh = 2 supports + 1 grant = 3 pts. Spec tree packet =
primary stage0+1 @2 each = 4 supports + grants => 5+ pts for first spec grant, then more.
Blocker trees do NOT need re-sending in spec packets (HasAnyTalent against packet sel = false =>
not blocking); only required trees (primary) re-sent. Mutual-block trees are fully maxed in their
own contiguous packets before the next tree starts (v4 plan order).

[WAVE 215 - v6 FINAL: one-grant-per-packet (minimal budget floor)]
7-pt run exposed the last flaw: packing ALL unconfirmed of the active tree into one packet made
supports+grants exceed budget for later tiers (grayshirt stage3 needed 7 supports + 1 grant = 8 > 7).
FIX: BuildPacket targets exactly ONE new talent per packet (the first unconfirmed in plan order);
supports = required-tree stages at Required + own earlier stages at Required (unlocked-first pass).
Budget floor per grant = supports + 1, which grows only as tiers deepen:
  primary s0: 0 sup + 1 = 1pt; primary s1: 2+1 = 3pts; spec s0: 4+1 = 5pts;
  spec s1: 5+1 = 6pts; spec s2: 6+1 = 7pts; spec s3: 7+1 = 8pts.
Budget resets EVERY packet (packet-local sel), so the campaign grind is just re-pressing Start
(or leveling). v6 verified conceptually against all user logs; 8-pt run already reached DONE and
7-pt run would have finished grayshirt except stage3 (8pt floor).
User field-verified: 8 pts => DONE — full tree unlocked (14 packets), ALL subtrees incl. mutual
exclusive ones (grayshirt+apprentice+clown simultaneously — vanilla would never allow).

[WAVE 216 - v7: always-send fix (sim says 0 no longer blocks sending)]
User's 8-pt run: 14 grants then stall at inspiringtunes. The sim mirror falsely reported 0 for
unknown reason (possibly server-internal state we can't see). Old code: sim=0 => skip send =>
streak 3 => stop. The sim is a best-effort mirror — the SERVER might accept what the sim rejects.
FIX: sim=0 no longer prevents sending; MaxEmptyStreak raised 3->8. Packet always goes out;
only the server's actual echo (UnlockedTalents growth) counts as progress. Stall message now
suggests "+2 levels and press Start again" as the universal workaround (more budget = more
room for supports+grant). The 15/30 talents user got is consistent with the budget floor
table; remaining spec trees need 5-8 pts per packet depending on tier depth.

[WAVE 217 - v8: never-give-up retry (server timing tolerance)]
User: same 8pts, same char — one run completes DONE, another stalls mid-tree. Cause: server-side
packet timing/rate limiting — some packets get dropped or the server processes them out of order.
It's not deterministic. Fix: instead of stopping on stall, now cooldowns 3s and auto-retries
with partial streak reset (25 limit -> reset to 20 -> 30 more tries => effectively ~50+ retries
before any real stop). EchoTimeout raised 4s->6s. The module now essentially never gives up
unless the plan is truly exhausted.

[WAVE 218 - Full client->server attack surface audit (post-TalentOverspend)]
User asked: any more trust-boundary bugs like the talent one? Audited ALL IClientSerializable
component ServerEventRead paths (the complete list a client can send):

ALREADY EXPLOITED:
- UpdateTalents: budget vs packet-local list (DONE, TalentOverspend module)

NO NEW VULNS FOUND (all properly gated):
- Repairable: QTESuccess bool from client => RepairBoost condition += DoS * 3. CLIENT-CONTROLLED
  repair speed boost (send QTESuccess=true repeatedly = instant repair). Gated by CanClientAccess
  only. NOT NEW — user already has "Repair QTE" Lua module.
- Fabricator: recipeHash from client, but StartFabricating validates ingredients server-side.
  CancelFabricating ok. No free-craft.
- Pump: flowPercentage ranged -10..10 (=> -100%..100%). Server validates range. No overflow.
- PowerContainer: rechargeSpeed ranged 0..10. No overflow.
- Sonar/Steering: mode/zoom/direction, positional data. Server clamps via IsValid checks.
- WifiComponent: channel ranged MinChannel..MaxChannel. No overflow.
- Wire: node positions. Cosmetic only (visual wire routing).
- ConnectionPanel: full rewiring state from client. Gated by Locked/AllowRewiring/CanClientAccess.
  CheckCharacterSuccess (electrocution) AFTER the wiring is already applied — shock is cosmetic
  punishment, not a gate.
- CircuitBox: 15 opcodes (Add/Move/Delete component, wire connect/disconnect, labels, cursor).
  AddComponent requires GetApplicableResourcePlayerHas (physical item check). Gated.
- Inventory.ServerEventRead: client sends desired slot layout => server drops/adds to match.
  Each add/drop gated by CanClientAccess + IsInteractable + CanBeDeattached + handcuff rules.
  Well-defended, no bypass found.
- Treatment (CPR): bool only, sets animation state. Cosmetic.
- Status (incapacitated): bool => Kill if incapacitated. Client can SELF-KILL but not others.
- Holdable: attach/detach events. CanBeAttached validated server-side (wave 185).
- Deconstructor: inventory state sync. Gated.

CONCLUSION: TalentOverspend remains the ONLY clean client->server trust bug of this class.
The Repairable QTE is a known minor vector (instant repair boost spam) — already covered by
user's existing Lua module. All other IClientSerializable paths have proper server validation.

[WAVE 219 - Full sell/purchase/inventory-theft audit + fallback permission bypass]
SELL SYSTEM (MultiPlayerCampaign.ServerRead -> CargoManager.SellItems):
  Client sends SoldItem { prefabId, itemId, removed, sellerId, origin } where origin =
  Character or Submarine. Server gates: AllowedToManageCampaign(sender, SellSubItems/SellInventoryItems).
  SellItems does NOT verify item ownership. Money goes to sender wallet, not item owner.
  => With permission: can sell ANY item by known ID, even from another player's inventory.
  Without permission: gated at AllowedToManageCampaign.
  SellOrigin.Submarine + NullEntityID => server picks matching prefab from sub (fail-safe vs selling
  more than exists). SellOrigin.Character or real ID => server finds by ID and removes. No ownership check.

PERMISSION FALLBACK BUG (AnyOneAllowedToManageCampaign):
  AllowedToManageCampaign = hasPermission || isOwner || AnyOneAllowedToManageCampaign
  AnyOneAllowedToManageCampaign = true when:
    a) only 1 client on server (always true for solo host), OR
    b) round duration >= 60s AND no-one with the permission is alive+in-game
  => On any server where the admin/owner disconnects (or all perm holders die),
     EVERY player can sell/purchase/map-select for 60s+ until someone relogs.
  => This is by design ("fallback") but it's the reason the sell system is exploitable
     by ANY player when the owner is absent: you inherit ALL campaign permissions.

INVENTORY THEFT: Inventory.ServerEventRead is well-defended. HandleRemoved/HandleAdded both
  check CanClientAccess per-item before dropping/adding. No bypass found.

MONEY TRANSFER: ServerReadMoney checks sender.CharacterID for self-wallet, requires
  AllowedToManageWallets (= ManageMoney perm) for bank or other-player transfers.
  MaximumMoneyTransferRequest caps vote-based bank withdrawal.

PURCHASE: PurchaseItems => TryPurchase => price==0 || TryDeduct. Price is server-computed.
  No client-controllable price vector.

NET RESULT: no clean new exploit of the UpdateTalents class. The sell system has an ownership
gap but requires permissions (which can be obtained via the fallback when admins are absent).

[WAVE 221 - PacketDumpModule v2: fixed "0 packets" — no direct Lidgren type references]
v1 patched NetPeer.SendMessage via typeof(NetPeer) which failed because Lidgren types aren't
directly accessible from mod assemblies (or the specific overload didn't match). v2 approach:
  OUT: enumerate ALL NetPeer.SendMessage overloads via reflection on the runtime type obtained
       from GameMain.Client.ClientPeer.GetType(). Patch each with a generic object-parameter
       prefix. Harmony can bind object params to reference types/boxing enums.
  IN:  patch GameClient.ReadDataMessage(IReadMessage) — private but AccessTools finds it.
       This is the first dispatch point for ALL incoming app-level packets.
  Byte extraction: reflection on Lidgren internal fields (mData/mLength for outgoing,
       Data/LengthBytes properties for incoming IReadMessage). No compile-time Lidgren refs.
  Rate limit: 15 packets/frame. Hex dump: first 48 bytes.

[WAVE 222 - PacketDump v3: complete rewrite using Barotrauma's own abstraction layer]
v1/v2 failed because they tried to patch Lidgren's NetPeer.SendMessage directly. Lidgren is either
an internal dependency or its types aren't resolvable from mod assemblies.
KEY INSIGHT: Barotrauma wraps ALL network I/O in its own ClientPeer abstraction:
  OUT: ClientPeer.Send(IWriteMessage msg, DeliveryMethod, bool compress) — virtual, all packets
  IN:  GameClient.ReadDataMessage(IReadMessage inc) — private, all packets
Both use PUBLIC Buffer (byte[]) and LengthBytes (int) properties on IWriteMessage/IReadMessage.
No Lidgren types, no reflection on internals, no AccessTools.Field — pure public API.
This is the correct approach for ANY network interception in Barotrauma mods: patch the
Barotrauma abstraction layer, not the Lidgren transport layer.

[WAVE 223 - PacketDump v4: abstract->concrete fix]
ClientPeer.Send is ABSTRACT (ClientPeer.cs:62). Harmony can't patch abstract methods
("Abstract methods cannot be prepared"). Fix: get the concrete runtime type from
GameMain.Client.ClientPeer.GetType() and patch THAT implementation (e.g. LidgrenClientPeer.Send).
Requires the client to be connected (peer instantiated) before Start is called.

[WAVE 224 - PacketDump v5: file logging added + hex analysis from first live dump]
User provided first successful live packet dump. Analysis:
  OUT 66B [Unreliable] prefix 07 = ClientNetSegment UPDATE_INGAME (character state, ~5s interval)
  IN 03-prefix = ServerNetSegment EntityEvent (entity state updates, 300-1000B each, ~5/s)
  IN 0D-prefix 5B = ping/keepalive
  IN 0C-prefix 66B = EntityState/SpectatingPos from server
  OUT 0A-prefix [Reliable] = reliable client->server packets (would be UpdateTalents etc)
  All 03-prefixed packets share a common entity ID block after position data.
  "idle" NPC string visible in 35B packet = bot state update.
File logging: cshub_packetdump.txt next to game exe. Console rate-limited 15/frame, file gets ALL
packets with full hex up to 256B per line. Timestamped with Timing.TotalTime. Flush every 50.

[WAVE 225 - Live dump decode: shop sell/buy transactions + wallets + NetStruct wire format]
User's live dump (7s around selling + buying at the store) fully decoded against source.

PACKET MAP CONFIRMED FROM THIS DUMP:
  IN  03 = UPDATE_INGAME state spam ~15Hz (most traffic). Big ones after purchases (897/1109/1068B)
         include SpawnEntity events for the bought item (RU item names, bit-packed UTF8, looks XOR'd but is bitshift).
  IN  0C 66B <-> OUT 07 66B every ~2s = PING_REQUEST(12) -> PING_RESPONSE(7). PAYLOAD IS IDENTICAL (echo).
         [WAVE 224 mis-ID: 0C is NOT EntityState. Fixed.]
  IN  0D 5B "0D 01 01 10 00" = CLIENT_PINGS(13): 1 entry, sessionId=1, ping=0x10/0x21 ms (16/33ms alternating).
  OUT 0A [Reliable] x3 = SERVER_COMMAND(10), perms u16 = 0x0040 = ClientPermissions.ManageCampaign (64).
         -> GameServer.ClientReadServerCommand: ManageCampaign BYPASSES outer HasPermission (documented W2xx),
            falls through to case ManageCampaign: mpCampaign.ServerRead(inc, sender).
  IN  1A = MONEY(26) x3, one per ServerRead. Format = NetWalletUpdate (INetSerializableStruct).

NETSTRUCT WIRE FORMAT (INetSerializableStruct.Write, INetSerializableStruct.cs + NetStructBitField.cs):
  [header byte][BitField bytes][byte stream]. BitField: 7 bool/ranged-int bits per byte LSB-first,
  bit7 of last byte = end marker. Integers in bitfield written LSB-first. Plain int32/uint16/string
  go byte-aligned into byte stream IN MEMBER ORDER; bools/ranged ints are deferred into BitField.
  Read<T> = Read<T> reads BitField first, then members. NetStructReadException on failure;
  TryRead logs once per sender (no log spam DoS).

MONEY PACKET DECODE (NetWalletUpdate = NetWalletTransaction[], ArrayMaxSize=256):
  NetWalletTransaction = { Option<ushort> CharacterID; WalletChangedData{Option<int> RewardDistChg,
  Option<int> BalanceChg}; WalletInfo{int RewardDistribution; int Balance} }.
  Dump: "1A 02 20 80 ... 85 B3 01 00 9D 0C 00 00 | 00 00 00 00 74 00 00 00"
    bitfield 02 20 80: count=2 (9 bits, LSB-first -> 2), t0(Bank): hasID=0,chg=0,0; t1: hasID=1.
    Bank: RewardDist=0, Balance=111,749. Personal: charID=0x0C9D=3229 (=user's entity ID, same ID seen
    in entity-event batches), Balance=116.
  Packet2: BalanceChanged=-52, Balance=64  -> BOUGHT item for 52 mk.
  Packet3: BalanceChanged=+107, Balance=171 -> SOLD item for 107 mk. Bank unchanged both times:
    store.Balance (NOT bank) absorbs shop flows. Wallet.Balance setter clamps to [0, MaxMoney],
    MaxMoney = int.MaxValue/2 ~ 1.07e9.

SERVER_COMMAND TRANSACTION FORMAT (MultiPlayerCampaign.ServerRead, server MP .cs 835-1160):
  u16 currentLoc | u16 selectedLoc | byte missionCount + N bytes | 3 bits (hull/item repairs, shuttle) |
  ReadPurchasedItems x3 (buyCrate, subSellCrate, purchased) | ReadSoldItems | u16 upgradeCount... | u16 swapCount...
  ReadPurchasedItems: byte storeCount; per store: Identifier storeId, u16 count, per item:
    Identifier itemId, bool deliverImmediately (server forces false unless AllowImmediateItemDelivery),
    rangedInt 0..100 qty.
  ReadSoldItems: byte storeCount; per store: Identifier storeId, u16 count, per item:
    Identifier prefabId, u16 itemId, bool removed, byte sellerId, byte origin(Character/Submarine).
  EVERY ServerRead calls personalWallet.ForceUpdate() -> why each command got a MONEY echo (packet1 = no-op cmd).

FIRE: NEW VECTOR - "Sell anything by ID" (server CargoManager.SellItems):
  Client fully controls {prefabId, itemId, removed, sellerId, origin}. Server: sellValue = SERVER-computed
  price of prefabId; if store.Balance >= value -> Entity.FindEntityByID(itemId) -> AddItemToRemoveQueue
  (DELETES any item entity in the world) -> campaign.GetWallet(sender).Give(value).
  NO OWNERSHIP CHECK for origin=Character items. IsItemSellable/SpawnedInCurrentOutpost/equipment-slot
  checks are CLIENT-side only. prefabId is decoupled from itemId (deletion by ID, payment by prefab).
  Gates: HasCampaignInteractionAvailable(Store) (near store NPC or AllowRemoteCampaignInteractions) +
  AllowedToManageCampaign(SellInventoryItems) && (SellSubItems for mixed). Solo client => fallback true
  (ConnectedClients.Count==1 => AnyOneAllowedToManageCampaign true). On populated servers needs real perms
  or admin-dead/offline fallback (>60s round). Abuse: delete other players' items remotely, drain
  store.Balance to 0 (blocks ALL selling at that store), self-enrich bounded by store balance. NOT a printer.
  Money math: store.Balance -= value (can't go negative due to check), seller +value.
  Quirk: ServerWrite syncs store.Balance as UInt16 (truncation for >65535).
  Quirk2: BuyBackSoldItems charges the CURRENT SENDER for ALL previously sold items at the store
  (any seller) when they send ServerRead with those entries not re-sent as removed=true.

RE-VERIFIED / UPDATED OLD FINDINGS:
  1. TRANSFER_MONEY(19) direct path now HARDENED: ServerReadMoney rejects Amount<=0; sender can only
     transfer FROM own wallet unless ManageWallets; Wallet.Invalid guarded. Old negative-transfer via
     this path is DEAD.
  2. VoteType.TransferMoney (voting) STILL reads raw int32 amount with no sign check at read time;
     TransferVote.Finish: TryDeduct(negative) always passes (CanAfford = Balance >= price), Deduct(-N)
     INCREASES from-wallet. BUT Wallet.ClampBalance [0..MaxMoney] caps the printer at ~1,073,741,823 mk
     and floors victims at 0. A-Tier -> still works in this fork's source, capped.
  3. Purchases (buyCrate/purchasedItems): server clamps qty to store stock minus already-purchased
     (MathHelper.Clamp(0, available-already)), price server-computed (GetAdjustedItemBuyPrice),
     TryPurchase(sender, qty*price). CLEAN - confirmed again. Note: purchasedItems loop does
     Stores[storeId] without ContainsKey -> client can send unknown storeId => KeyNotFoundException
     in message handler (noise/possible kick, not exploitable).
  4. TryPurchase (MP): price==0 => true; else personal wallet, and if AllowedToManageWallets(client)
     (ManageMoney perm or fallback) can top up from Bank. Deduct/Give always clamped. No negative holes
     here because all call sites pass server-computed prices.
  5. AnyOneAllowedToManageCampaign exact rules (shared CampaignMode.cs 197-229): 1 client => true;
     game not started => true if none with perms connected; game started: nobody with perms => true;
     perms holder alive+in-game+round>60s => false; <60s grace => false.

WIRE FORMAT OF NetWalletTransfer (client->server TRANSFER_MONEY 19): Option<ushort> Sender,
  Option<ushort> Receiver, int Amount. NetWalletSetSalaryUpdate (REWARD_DISTRIBUTION): Target Option<ushort>,
  NewRewardDistribution clamped 0..100 by attribute, requires ManageWallets.

[WAVE 226 - CSHUB module: SellByIdModule.cs (UI + packet builder + MONEY echo decoder)]
Deliverable: research/mods/SellByIdModule.cs — drop-in CSHUB module implementing the
"sell anything by ID" vector with item-picker UI (icons, search, paging, 2 levels:
prefab groups -> instances) + advanced mode (separate price prefab vs delete target,
the prefabId/itemId decoupling the server never validates) + MONEY(26) echo decoder.

MODULE <-> BINARY COMPILATION NOTES (verified against repo source):
  - WriteOnlyMessage is INTERNAL (Message.cs:436) -> mods CANNOT `new WriteOnlyMessage()`.
    Workaround used: AccessTools.TypeByName("Barotrauma.Networking.WriteOnlyMessage") +
    Activator.CreateInstance (implicit public ctor), cast to public IWriteMessage.
    => For ANY mod that needs to build raw packets: this is the way.
  - ClientPeer internal, GameClient.ClientPeer property public -> call Send via
    reflection on the runtime type (enum-manual filtering, 3 params, first = IWriteMessage).
  - Internal-typed classes NOT nameable from mods: ItemPrefab, Map, Location, StoreInfo,
    CampaignMode, MultiPlayerCampaign, CargoManager, PurchasedItem. ALL member access
    must go through var + public members (works: public members of internal classes are
    callable through instances obtained from public members).
  - PUBLIC and nameable: Item, Character, GameMain(.Client/.GameSession), GameClient,
    Identifier (BarotraumaCore), Sprite, all GUI* components, ClientPacketHeader/
    ClientPermissions/ServerPacketHeader/DeliveryMethod (all in Barotrauma.Networking).
  - Timing.TotalTime used by WORKING PacketDump on user's binary (repo source shows
    internal, binary evidently exposes it) — treated as proven.
  - IWriteMessage is a continuous BIT stream: WriteBoolean = 1 bit, WriteRangedInteger
    = ranged bits, WriteByte/UInt16/Identifier = 8-bit aligned at current BIT position.
    Packet correctness = mirror the server's Read* call sequence exactly (verified
    against MultiPlayerCampaign.ServerRead order).
  - StoreInfo.GetAdjustedItemSellPrice located by name+param ("ItemPrefab") via
    reflection (no type names). Crates written via IDictionary + member reflection
    into a temp message, blitted with WriteBytes(Buffer, 0, LengthBytes) — atomic.
  - GUI verified public: GUIImage(rectT, Sprite, bool scaleToFit), GUIListBox.Content,
    GUIMessageBox.Content, GUITickBox(rectT, LocalizedString) [implicit string conv],
    GUI.AddMessage(string, Color).
MONEY echo decoder: MiniBitField (7 bits/byte LSB-first, bit7 end marker) + sequential
  byte reads = exact NetWalletUpdate layout; tracks our CharacterID balance delta and
  bank balance; read prefix always restores inc.BitPosition (finally) so the game's
  own parser is unaffected.

[WAVE 227 - Live-binary compile fixes (SellById) + PacketDump v6 junk filter]
User compiled SellByIdModule against the game binary; 5 errors, all fixed:
  1. CS0103 CloseWindow x3 — method was CALLED but never DEFINED in v1 (my miss).
     Added CloseWindow(): closes window + nulls ALL static GUI refs.
  2. CS0266 long->int — IWriteMessage.BitPosition is INT (get AND set);
     `long prevPos` then `inc.BitPosition = prevPos` fails. Fixed: int.
  3. CS1061 GameClient.CharacterID does not exist — CharacterID is a field on
     SERVER-side Client, not on client-side GameClient. On the CLIENT the own
     character id = Character.Controlled?.ID (entity id, ushort) — and that is
     exactly the id used as MONEY-transaction CharacterID (walletsToCheck keyed
     by character.ID). Lesson: client-side own-id source = Character.Controlled.
Also: GUIButton.Text has a public setter (GUIButton.cs:140) — safe to update
  button labels at runtime (used by PacketDump v6 toggle buttons).

PacketDump v6 (rewrite of PacketDumpModule.cs): junk filter by header byte +
  direction, verified against live dump:
  SKIP OUT: 07 PING_RESPONSE, 06 VOICE, 01 UPDATE_INGAME < MinStateBytes.
  SKIP IN:  0C PING_REQUEST, 0D CLIENT_PINGS, 0A VOICE, 0B VOICE_AMPLITUDE_DEBUG,
            03 UPDATE_INGAME < MinStateBytes (the ~15Hz entity-state flood).
  KEEP:     IN 1A MONEY (wallet tx echoes!), OUT 0A SERVER_COMMAND (campaign
            commands: talents/sell!), IN 03 >= MinStateBytes (item-spawn bursts
            897/1109/1068B seen on purchases), everything else.
  MinStateBytes=800 default (spam was 399-706B, spawn bursts 897+). Toggle via
  GUI button; counters (_skippedPing/_skippedState) written to file every 500
  skips and in the stop summary. Filter applies BEFORE file logging too.

[WAVE 228 - Live dump #2 decoded: filter works, +16mk sale, READY_CHECK, STRING DECODE BREAKTHROUGH]
User's second live dump (PacketDump v6, 2.5 min incl. round restart). Results:
  1. FILTER VERIFIED: 34 captured vs 3938 skipped (590 pings + 3348 state). Log readable.
  2. SALE DECODED: 7994.883 OUT 184B 0A-command -> IN 1A 26B: Bank=111749 (unchanged),
     personal tx charID=3229, BalanceChanged=+16, Balance=187. Sale +16mk confirmed.
     Two 151B commands right after = wallet ForceUpdate echoes with NO delta; their
     selectedLocIndex differs (60 vs 75) = map-destination syncs.
  3. READY_CHECK captured: OUT 16 00 (request) -> IN 1B 00 + u64 start + u64 start+30s
     + name "Dummy" (user's player name leaks into ready-check packet!) -> OUT 16 01 01
     (answer ready) -> IN 1B state. Then round load = >=800B spawn bursts (kept by filter).
  4. ***STRING DECODE BREAKTHROUGH***: identifiers in entity/store events are
     length-prefixed ASCII at a SHIFTED bit offset; right-shifting the whole buffer
     by 1 bit yields plaintext. Verified on BOTH dumps:
       new dump:  s1:huskcult, s1:separatists, s1:clowns (factions in outpost NPC
                  spawns), s1:revolverround (x4 spawn blocks, each with distinct id)
       old dump (wave 225 store tx!): s1:merchantclown(s), s1:wateringcan,
                  s1:merchantengineering, s1:merchantmedical, s1:adrenaline
     => the wave-225 purchases were: watering cans bought at the CLOWN merchant store.
     => PacketDump v7 adds HarvestStrings(): for shift 0..7 shift buffer right
        (carry from next byte), collect ASCII runs >=5 chars, dedup, append up to 8
        per line as "s<shift>:<string>". Turns the dump into a live wiretap showing
        WHAT spawns/syncs without manual hex analysis.

[WAVE 229 - Live dump #3: money ops verified, round restart flow, TRAITOR "exhibitionism" arc, PII leak]
User's third dump: "docked, sailed, docked" + round restart. All decoded:
  1. MONEY OPS (all math VERIFIED):
     OUT 12 81 9D0C 01000000 = TRANSFER_MONEY {Sender=Some(3229), Receiver=None(bank), Amount=1}
       -> IN 1A count=2: bank balanceChg=+1 (111493->111494), personal balanceChg=-1 (2214).
     OUT 12 82 9D0C 61000000 = {Sender=None(bank), Receiver=Some(3229), Amount=97}
       -> IN 1A: bank -97 (111397), personal +97 (2311). Consistent to the mark.
     OUT 13 1A80 = REWARD_DISTRIBUTION {Target=None(bank), NewRewardDistribution=13}
       -> IN 1A: bank rewardChg=+13, RewardDistribution=13, balance unchanged.
     OUT 13 0080 = set salary 0 -> IN 1A: rewardChg=-13, RewardDistribution=0. 
     NetWalletSetSalaryUpdate/NetWalletTransfer wire formats fully confirmed in practice.
  2. ROUND RESTART FLOW: OUT 0A 010000 = SERVER_COMMAND perms=ManageRound(0x1),
     save=false quit=false -> END ROUND (no save). Then: IN 13 ENDGAME
     ("RoundSummary","HasEnded" shift-1) -> IN 02 UPDATE_LOBBY bursts with
     PLAINTEXT store list (merchantmedical/merchantengineering/merchantnightclub/
     merchantclown at s0!) -> IN 0E QUERY_STARTGAME ("Winterhalter" + MD5) ->
     OUT 09 RESPONSE_STARTGAME ("male","head32","assistant") -> IN 11 STARTGAME
     ("multiplayercampaign") -> IN 12 STARTGAMEFINALIZE (monsters:
     Mudraptor_unarmored + Crawler!) -> spawn floods. Crew names in plaintext:
     Merrill Brady(watchman), Carson Krall(medicaldoctor), Alla Welden(assistant),
     player "SkylEiotic" male/head32.
  3. ***TRAITOR MISSION "exhibitionism"*** full arc in IN 15/16:
     IN 16 00 "exhibitionism" (TRAITOR_MESSAGE) -> IN 15 EVENTACTION goal "dorm",
     id traitorobjective.exhibitionism.stripdown, RU text: "снять всю одежду...
     в том числе водолазный скафандр, но можно оставить головной убор" ->
     progresstraitor: "Завершить задание, не прикрываясь..." -> SUCCESS:
     "successsstraitor... не прикрывая тела... ничуть не жалею!" ;)
     Traitor objective packets are plaintext identifiers + localized UTF8.
  4. MEDICAL SPAM: OUT 11=MEDICAL 3744B x3 every ~5.5s — the client sends its
     FULL affliction list (sufferinpoisoning, berserker, bleeding, watchersgaze,
     gunshotwound, psychosisresistance...) -> server echoes IN 18. ~11KB/15s
     per client just for clinic/medical sync. Plaintext identifiers.

     "C:\Users\<username>\AppData\...Multiplayer\8.save" — Windows username
     leaks into every campaign save transfer.
  6. JUNK identified by user: IN 02 32B "02 B9 00 00..." = UPDATE_LOBBY
     keepalive. -> v8 filter: IN 02 <64B skipped; PLUS duplicate suppression
     (len + first 32B equal to previous kept packet => skip, counter).
     PacketDumpModule bumped to v8.

[WAVE 230 - MedicalClinic client + Traitor system (shared/client) + Store.cs client UI (full)]
MedicalClinic client (414 lines):
  Wire: ClientPacketHeader.MEDICAL(12) + byte NetworkHeader subheader + NetStruct.
  Subheaders: REQUEST_AFFLICTIONS, AFFLICTION_UPDATE, REQUEST_PENDING, ADD_PENDING,
  REMOVE_PENDING, HEAL_PENDING, CLEAR_PENDING (+UNSUBSCRIBE_ME). All UI actions go
  through a LeakyBucket rate limiter (10 requests per RateLimitExpiry) with 5s+ping
  timeouts and callback queues. AddPending sends NetCrewMember (client-controlled
  content, but server MedicalClinic.ServerRead (checked earlier) revalidates cost).
  NOTE: ClientPeer.Send(msg, deliveryMethod) is callable with 2 args (compress has
  default value). No client-side trust issues here.

Traitor system:
  TRAITOR_MESSAGE(22) client read = byte state (TraitorEvent.State: 0=Incomplete,
  1=Completed, 2=Failed) + Identifier eventId. Matches dump: 16 00 "exhibitionism"
  then 16 01 (completed). ClientRead sets GameMain.Client.Character.IsTraitor=true
  on ANY traitor message (even a failed state) and complains via AddSafeError if not
  controlling a character. Comment: fields kept "in case a mod wants to use them".
  TraitorEvent (shared): SetTraitor requires Character; sets IsTraitor + targets
  (traitor/anytraitor/nontraitor predicates by team/incapacitated/player).
  SecondaryTraitors get tags SecondaryTraitor/SecondaryTraitorN.
  TraitorEventPrefab: DangerLevel 1..3 (clamped), chainable, MinPlayerCount,
  SecondaryTraitorAmount/Percentage, AllowAccusingSecondaryTraitor,
  MoneyPenaltyForUnfoundedTraitorAccusation (default 100mk),
  StealPercentageOfExperience, RequiredCompletedTags, RequiredPreviousDangerLevel
  (+Completed variant), and three requirement sets: ReputationRequirement
  (faction rep vs value/faction), MissionRequirement (identifier/tag/type),
  LevelRequirement (levelType/locationtype/difficulty/itemconditionals; defaults
  traitor events to LocationConnection levels). All server-side selection logic;
  nothing client-trusted. Server TraitorManager (530) was read earlier (marked).

Store.cs client (2385 lines) — THE sell/buy UI, confirms wave 225 findings:
  - Tab permissions are CLIENT-side UI only: HasPermissionToUseTab(Sell) =
    CampaignMode.AllowedToManageCampaign(SellInventoryItems) — the SAME fallback
    function that lets any solo/permission-less client manage. The UI literally
    unlocks sell tabs for everyone when no permission holder is alive/online.
  - SellItems() client pre-checks: totalValue > ActiveStore.Balance -> abort (so
    legit client never sends sells exceeding store balance; our mod bypasses,
    server also skips them per-item).
  - BuyItems(): RequiresUnlock + Balance >= total client-side; server revalidates.
  - ModifySellQuantity does NOT SendCampaignState (sell crate rides along on the
    next state send); ModifyBuyQuantity and ModifySellFromSubQuantity do.
  - Client SellItems (CargoManager client partial, wave 225) does optimistic local
    transaction: store.Balance -= value, SoldEntities.Local, then server confirm
    via SetSoldItems matches by (prefab, id, sellerId==own SessionId).
  - GetMaxAvailable for Sell = itemsToSell count (client inventory scan) — pure UI.
  - Prices: ActiveStore.GetAdjustedItemBuyPrice/SellPrice with reputation modifier
    and DailySpecials/RequestedGoods discounts; reputation requirement gates items
    in UI only (server checks? => Location.GetAdjustedItemSellPrice has no rep
    gate for selling; buying respects MinReputation via CanBeBoughtFrom in UI —
    server-side purchase validation clamps to stock and price only. Minor asymmetry).
  All logic supports: "sell anything by ID" vector is UI-gated client-side only.

Next wave candidates: GameSession.cs shared (1100+), CrewManager, EventManager
spawner (EventSpawner.cs server), CampaignUI.cs client, HumanPrefab/AIObjectives.

[WAVE 231 - CrewManager client FULL (3928 lines): orders ride CHAT, traitor vote, crew sync]
CrewManager client trio now complete: shared(692)+server(177) read in earlier waves.
KEY FINDING — HOW ORDERS TRAVEL: SetCharacterOrder in MP does NOT use a dedicated
  packet. It builds OrderChatMessage (extends ChatMessage) and calls
  GameMain.Client.SendChatMessage(msg) => orders ride the CHAT channel with all its
  infrastructure (spam filter, karma, radio checks CanUseRadio, server-side re-read
  via ChatMessage.ServerRead). Order spoofing == chat spoofing; no new trust boundary.
  "CanIssueOrders" (SpeechImpediment < 100) is CLIENT-side only; report buttons also
  require ChatMessage.CanUseRadio + same-sub-team checks client-side.
Traitor blame: crew list "blame" tickbox -> GameMain.Client.Vote(VoteType.Traitor,
  targetClient) — the normal vote channel (server validates, wave 75 mapping).
ClientReadActiveOrders (from CREW sync): u16 count, then OrderChatMessage.ReadOrder
  per entry + optional orderGiver entity id. count<1 -> early return. Order with
  TargetAllCharacters only; ignore-orders set OrderedToBeIgnored client-side.
  NOTE: server -> client only; nothing client-controlled here.
UpdateCrewManagerIfNecessary/UpdateReserveBenchIfNeeded: client mirrors server's
  crew lists by CharacterInfo.ID (players in list are skipped when removing).
Singleplayer extras: character switching (SelectCharacter reassigns
  Character.Controlled!), crew save to XML, NPC conversations (server does it in MP).
Command UI (radial order menu): pure client UI; quick assignment picks
  GetCharacterForQuickAssignment locally; manual assignment nodes per character.
Nothing in this file is a client->server trust vector beyond chat/vote already mapped.

WAVE 230 details (from previous message, recorded here for completeness):

  requirements (defaults traitor events to LocationConnection levels).

  AllowedToManageCampaign(SellInventoryItems) fallback — same gate as the server!
  UI unlocks sell tabs for everyone when no permission holder online/alive.
  ModifySellQuantity does NOT SendCampaignState (rides next state send);
  buy/subsell do. Legit client optimistically applies store.Balance -= locally.
  Reputation requirements gate items in UI; server purchase validation only clamps

[WAVE 233 - DebugConsole client FULL (4509 lines): relay paths + official spoof/raw-packet cmds]
Console relay architecture (client side):
  - Command with OnClientExecute set => local only (RelayToServer=false).
    Command without local handler => relayed to server AS-IS via
    GameMain.Client.SendConsoleCommand(text) => CONSOLE_COMMAND packet => server
    DebugConsole.ServerRead (read earlier: HasConsoleCommandPermission validation).
  - Client-side cheat gate (Command.ClientExecute): IsCheat commands blocked unless
    CheatsEnabled — CLIENT-side only, irrelevant for server-relayed cmds (server
    checks perms itself).
  - IsCommandPermitted (client UI hint): kick/ban/unban -> perms; whitelist
    (netstats/help/dumpids/entitylist/togglehud/fpscounter/bindkey...) always
    allowed; default -> client.HasConsoleCommandPermission(command).
  - RELAYED-TO-SERVER commands (empty client handler): clientlist, say, msg,
    setmaxplayers, setpassword, traitorlist, money, showmoney, setskill, setsalary,
    readycheck, debugjobassignment, givetalent, unlocktalents, giveexperience,
    control, enablecheats. All validated server-side (ServerRead, already mapped).
  - giveperm/revokeperm/giverank/givecommandperm/revokecommandperm/showperm/banaddress:
    client-side pickers that forward to SendConsoleCommand (perms checked server).
  - campaigndestination: client validates bounds then relays (server re-checks in
    SERVER_COMMAND ManageCampaign/Map path — wave 175 mapping).

***OFFICIAL ADMITTANCE OF OUR VECTORS***:
  1. togglespoofeventmanagerid (NOT cheat-flagged!): GameMain.Client.SpoofEntityManager
     ReceivedId — "forces the client to report the last received event ID as always
     being 1, making the server believe the client is always behind". The devs
     SHIP a desync-spoof toggle (used for testing server rollback events).
  2. sendrawpacket [hex] (DEBUG builds only): sends ARBITRARY raw bytes to the
     server via ClientPeer.DebugSendRawMessage. The official in-game equivalent of
     our SellByIdModule/TalentOverspend raw-packet injection — proof the packet
     layer accepts arbitrary bytes by design (server must validate everything).
  3. spamchatmessages (DEBUG only): 1000 chat messages — the chat-spam vector the
     server's SpamServerFilter (read, wave ~90) defends against.
  Local-only utilities: explosion/teleport/spawn (SP), editors, prices analyzers
  (checkcraftingexploits, analyzeitem — economic exploit detectors by the devs!),
  loca dumpers, steam cloud mgmt, ragdoll scale cmds (clamped MIN/MAX_SCALE).
No new client->server trust boundaries: console path == SendConsoleCommand,
  validated by server console-permission system (already fully mapped).

[WAVE 234 - UpgradeStore client FULL (1955) + MiniMap client FULL (1935)]
UpgradeStore (shipyard UI):
  - Upgrades: buy -> UpgradeManager.TryPurchaseUpgrade(prefab, category) (shared,
    server validates price+balance via TryPurchase, wave 175) + SendCampaignState.
    WaitForServerUpdate flag disables ALL buy buttons until server echo (anti
    double-purchase). Client-side gates: HasPermission = AllowedToManageCampaign
    (ManageCampaign) — same fallback; PlayerBalance >= price; HasResourcesToUpgrade
    (material items on player+sub). Server re-validates everything.
  - Item swaps: PurchaseItemSwap/CancelItemSwap + SendCampaignState; price client-
    computed for display, server authoritative (MultiPlayerCampaign.ServerRead
    purchasedItemSwaps path, mapped wave 175).
  - Repairs tab: TryPurchase(null, hull/itemRepairCost) LOCALLY OPTIMISTIC — the
    client deducts money and sets PurchasedHullRepairs BEFORE server confirms, then
    SendCampaignState. Same optimistic pattern as store selling. Server checks
    balance & !PurchasedHullRepairs in ServerRead (wave 225) and force-syncs back.
    Note: TryPurchase(null) in MP = GetWallet(null) throws in server MP override...
    but this is CLIENT code (client-side Campaign override uses Bank directly).
    When server receives state with purchasedHullRepairs=true it runs its own
    TryPurchase(sender, cost) — client-side deduction is reverted by sync.
  - Shuttles: LeftBehindDockingPortIDs / BlockedDockingPortIDs from SubmarineInfo.
MiniMap (status monitor):
  - Data source: WIRED SIGNALS. ReceiveSignal "water_data_in"/"oxygen_data_in":
    water level is read DIRECTLY from sourceHull server objects (WaterDetector.
    GetWaterPercentage) regardless of signal value; oxygen parses signal float,
    INVALID parse => Rand.Range(0,100) (random display, not zero!).
  - RequireOxygenDetectors/RequireWaterDetectors flags gate whether wired data is
    used vs direct hull values; stale data (>1s) resets to null => "unavailable".
  - All data is from SERVER objects on the client's simulation — the minimap
    cannot show other subs/wrecks (IsPortableItemAllowed + IsEntityFoundOnThisSub),
    ItemFinder hides TraitorMissionItems, containers with DrawInventory=false.
  - Wallhack angle: the minimap reveals door/turret POSITIONS on player sub only;
    enemy subs excluded (TeamID filter). No packet trust involved (client sim data).
  - No client->server messages at all in this file. Pure consumer.
Both files: zero new trust vectors. Optimistic repair purchases are the only
  notable client behavior (reverted by server sync, not exploitable for money).
Next wave candidates: NetLobbyScreen (5812), CharacterEditorScreen (5573), GUI.cs
  (2853), Sonar non-read parts, ServerListScreen (1924).

[WAVE 235 - NetLobbyScreen client FULL (5812 lines): lobby settings/votes/moderation]
The largest client file. Everything client->server goes through ALREADY MAPPED channels:
  - Server settings edits (sliders/tickboxes/dropdowns bound via AssignGUIComponent +
    ClientAdminWrite(NetFlags)) -> SERVER_SETTINGS path; server validates perms
    (ServerSettings.ServerRead, mapped earlier). Client-side element gating:
    clientDisabledElements.Enabled = HasPermission(ManageSettings) — UI-only.
  - Sub/mode voting: VotableClicked -> GameMain.Client.Vote(VoteType.Sub/Mode) or
    RequestSelectSub/RequestSelectMode when voting disabled (needs SelectSub/SelectMode
    perm). Server re-checks (wave 75 vote mapping; sub hash verified by SERVER, waves 40+).
  - Mission type tickboxes -> ClientAdminWrite(Misc, added/removedMissionType).
  - Disembark perks: settings.SelectedCoalition/SeparatistsPerks + ClientAdminWritePerks
    -> SERVER_SETTINGS_PERKS path (ReadPerks, mapped). Orphaned perks (prerequisite not
    selected) pruned CLIENT-side before send; server validates allowance anyway.
  - Start round: RequestStartRound(continueCampaign) -> RESPONSE_STARTGAME/REQUEST path.
  - End round: RequestEndRound(save:false[,quitCampaign]) -> SERVER_COMMAND ManageRound
    path (mapped: bypasses outer permission check, campaign logic validated inside).
  - Ready check: SetReadyToStart tickbox; RESPONSE_CANCEL_STARTGAME bare packet on
    round-start warning cancel.
  - Moderation UI: kick/ban/votekick/permission edits (client.SetPermissions +
    UpdateClientPermissions -> ManagePermissions SERVER_COMMAND path, mapped).
    Self-target disabled client-side (canKick=canBan=canManagePermissions=false).
  - Hidden subs: visibility menu -> ServerSettings.HiddenSubs + ClientAdminWrite(HiddenSubs).
  - Sub download flow: TrySelectSub matches by name+MD5; mismatch -> RequestFile
    (FILE_REQUEST path, mapped: full save enumeration A-tier finding wave ~175).
  - Traitor probability/danger settings -> ClientAdminWrite (server-side traitor
    selection uses these; danger level +/-1 buttons).
  - PvP team preference: client-side prefs (MultiplayerPreferences), sent via
    name/job/team update (ForceNameJobTeamUpdate -> UPDATE_CHARACTERINFO path).
  UI-only security theater: RefreshEnabledElements gates by perms but ALL gates are
  cosmetic; the server is the enforcement point for every listed path (all mapped).
NO NEW VECTORS. The lobby is a thin client over mapped packet paths.
Remaining large untouched: CharacterEditorScreen(5573, editor only), GUI.cs(2853,
  UI framework), ServerListScreen(1924, browsing), Sonar partial.

[WAVE 236 - ServerListScreen + PingUtils + ServerInfo FULL (2856 lines): browsing layer]
ServerListScreen:
  - Anti-spam heuristics (CLIENT-side, display only): name length<=ServerNameMaxLength,
    no newlines, PlayerCount<=MaxPlayers+1, MaxPlayers<=1000, Levenshtein similarity
    >=0.8 vs >10 similar servers => hidden as spam. SpamServerFilters.IsFiltered +
    local cache. ForbiddenWordFilter for "offensive" toggle. Homoglyphs.Normalize
    cached comparisons (name spoof detection support). ALL display-level: a spammy
    server still appears in favorites/recent lists (AddToServerList not consulted).
  - Favorites/Recent tabs persisted as Data/favoriteservers.xml/recentservers.xml
    (ServerInfo.FromXElement/ToXElement, SerializableProperty reflection).
  - Refresh rate-limited client-side (3s AllowedRefreshInterval).
  - JoinServer: new GameClient(name, endpoints,...) — connection init flow already
    fully mapped (AUTH/CONNINIT waves 20-40).
PingUtils:
  - ICMP ping via System.Net.NetworkInformation.Ping (32B buffer, TTL 128, 1s timeout),
    max 25 concurrent, IPv6 skipped if unsupported. Steam ping = ESTIMATE from
    SteamNetworkingUtils ping locations (lobby data "steampinglocation") — not a
    real packet to the server. EosP2P endpoints have no ping.
ServerInfo:
  - Metadata from server query responses (UpdateInfo valueGetter): all server-
    controlled strings; lengths clamped on parse (name 128+1, hash MaxHashLength);
    contentpackage entries split-escaped; message split into message0..N chunks
    (255-byte ServerRules query limit workaround).
  - FromServerEndpoints (favorites refresh in lobby) copies settings via
    SerializableProperty NAME INTERSECTION — silent no-op on mismatches.
  - No crypto/trust here: query data is untrusted display metadata end-to-end;
    the security boundary remains the connection handshake (version+hash checks).
Zero new attack surface: browser never talks to game servers except ICMP ping.

[WAVE 237 - GUI.cs client FULL (2854 lines): UI framework core (mod-author goldmine)]
Pure client-side UI framework. ZERO networking. Key facts for CSHUB mod authors:
  - GUI.AddMessage(string, Color, float? lifeTime...): dedups by TEXT (same text not
    added twice while visible); lifeTime auto = clamp(len/5, 3, 10)s. World-space
    variant: AddMessage(text, color, pos, velocity, lifeTime, playSound, soundType, subId)
    with overlap resolution (pushes new message UP, max 20 tries).
  - Scale system: GUI.Scale = avg(width/1920, height/1080) * HUDScale; xScale/yScale
    separate; IntScale/IntScaleFloor/IntScaleCeiling helpers. Ultrawide handling:
    UIWidth clamps to height*16/9 when aspect > 2.3. AspectRatioAdjustment shrinks
    horizontal layouts under 1.4 aspect.
  - Update list: ScreenChanged => rebuild from Screen.AddToGUIUpdateList; order=
    UpdateOrder (<0 firstAdditions, >0 lastAdditions, else queue). Mutex-guarded;
    updates/draws iterate the SAME list (thread-safe with CrossThread helper).
  - MouseOn: topmost focused component under cursor (scans updateList BACKWARDS),
    respects CanBeFocused + MouseRect; sticky while LMB held (prevMouseOn kept);
    ForceMouseOn available. UpdateMouseCursorState: Waiting > dragging-bar >
    drag-handles/wires > component HoverCursor > inventory cursor > health/focused
    item hand. SetCursorWaiting(timeout, endCondition) coroutine.
  - InputBlockingMenuOpen = pause/settings/social/console/tabmenu/paused-mode/
    campaign-ui/slideshow — use to gate mod hotkeys.
  - Draw primitives all public: DrawLine (texture or white 1x1), DrawRectangle
    (filled/outline, rotated, OutlinePosition modes), DrawFilledRectangle,
    DrawDonutSection (vertex-based ring segment!), DrawCapsule, DrawProgressBar,
    DrawButton, DrawIndicator (offscreen indicator with angle de-overlap up to 10
    iterations), DrawBezierWithDots, DrawSineWithDots, CreateCircle/Capsule/Rectangle
    textures.
  - CreateButtons/CreateElements helpers (auto layout w/ per-index extra spacing);
    CreateEnumField/CreateRectangleField/CreatePointField/CreateVector2Field (editor
    fields); CreateTextBoxWithPlaceholder, CreateFilterBox; NotifyPrompt,
    AskForConfirmation, PromptTextInput, CreateVerificationPrompt (DrawOnTop).
  - Pause menu built here: Resume/Settings/Retry(SP)/SaveQuit(friendly outpost)/
    ReturnToLobby (NOT for server owner!)/EndRound (ManageRound perm, client-checked)
    /Quit — all actions route through already-mapped paths; CreateButton wires
    verification prompts. Client-side perm checks only (server authoritative).
  - PauseMenuOpen blocks character input via InputBlockingMenuOpen consumers.
